aleCenters_Lang]  WHERE [SaleCenterId] = @SaleCenterId

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteStockSupplyWithDependencies]








/*RZ14020914*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteStockSupplyWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteStockSupplyWithDependencies 1,1033,1065
*/
@StockSupplyId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------- Find BuyOrder ----------------------------*/
DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1, @Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''

/**/
DECLARE @BuyOrderId INT = NULL
SET @BuyOrderId = (SELECT TOP(1) BuyOrderID
				   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]  
				   WHERE [BuyOrderID] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(bords.[value],'0') AS INT)
										 FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
										 CROSS APPLY ((SELECT [value] FROM STRING_SPLIT([stsupIBuyOrderIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bords
										 WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId 
										 AND ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderIds],'') <>''))										 
IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0)/*Bord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-3'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
END
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/								
IF(@CanDelete = 1)
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRY
	/**/
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/*------------------------------------------------*/	
			/*------------ FilesDirectory ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
										WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] IN(SELECT FT.FileDirectoryTypeID FROM [dbo].[CrmFileDirectoryType] FT WHERE FT.FileDirectoryTypeOprId = 100)
										AND [TypeOprObjectId] = @StockSupplyId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] IN(SELECT FT.FileDirectoryTypeID FROM [dbo].[CrmFileDirectoryType] FT WHERE FT.FileDirectoryTypeOprId = 100)
			AND [TypeOprObjectId] = @StockSupplyId

			/*------------ FilesDirectory IN DyForm ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
										WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] IN(-60,-65)
										AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																 FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																 WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
																 AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																				FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																				WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
																				OR [FOSysPartId] = 45)))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] IN(-60,-65)
			AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 45))
			/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
													FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
													WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																					FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																					WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																					AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																													WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
																													AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																																	WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
																																	OR [FOSysPartId] = 45))))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 45)))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 45)))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
			WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
			AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 45))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  
			WHERE FieldValueId IN (SELECT FormFieldValueId 
									FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
									WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
															WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
															AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																			WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
																			OR [FOSysPartId] = 45)))
		
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
									WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
									AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
													WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
													OR [FOSysPartId] = 45))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
			WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
									WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
									AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
													WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
													OR [FOSysPartId] = 45))
			/**/		
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 45 
							OR [FOSysPartId] = 45)

			/*------------ WSVerificationRequests -----------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
													FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
													WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																					FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																					WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
																					AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = @StockSupplyId))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = @StockSupplyId)

			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = @StockSupplyId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
			WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 45
			AND [VerificationReqObjectId] = @StockSupplyId
			

			/*------------ StockSupply ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang] 
			WHERE StockSupplyItemId IN(SELECT I.StockSupplyItemId
								       FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] I
									   WHERE I.StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId)
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
			WHERE StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId

			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply_Lang]
			WHERE StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
			WHERE StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			SET @Result = '1'
		COMMIT
	/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
	/**/
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SET @Result = '0'
	/**/
	END CATCH
/**/
END
/*----------------------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @Result AS [Result]
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteWSBuyOrderItems]







/*RZ960203 For multi language*/
/*RZ14020925- Add remove bordid and bordItem from stockSupply*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteWSBuyOrderItems]
(		
/*
exec WSspDeleteWSBuyOrderItems
*/
@BuyOrderItemId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020925- Add remove bordid and bordItem from stockSupply*/
		DECLARE @CrsrStockSupplyItemId INT = NULL,@CrsrBordIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,@CrsrBordItemIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL		
		,@BuyOrderId INT = NULL
		SEt @BuyOrderId = (SELECT TOP(1) [BuyOrderId] FROm [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemId)
		DECLARE @bordIdTmp TABLE(
			bordId INT
		)
		DECLARE @bordItemdTmp TABLE(
			bordItemId INT
		)
		DECLARE CrsrUpStsupItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/	
			SELECT 
				 [StockSupplyItemId]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderIds]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds]					
			FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
			WHERE ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderIds],'') <> ''
			AND @BuyOrderId IN (SELECT 
									CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
								FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderIds],',')
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)															   								 							  		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrUpStsupItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrUpStsupItem INTO @CrsrStockSupplyItemId,@CrsrBordIds,@CrsrBordItemIds
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/			
			DELETE FROM @bordIdTmp
			DELETE FROM @bordItemdTmp
			/**/
			DECLARE @RemoveBord BIT = 0
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CrsrBordItemIds,'') <> '')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO @bordItemdTmp (bordItemId)
				SELECT 
					CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrBordItemIds,',')
				WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
				AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> @BuyOrderItemId
				
				SET @CrsrBordItemIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(bordItemId, '0') AS NVARCHAR(50))
										FROM  @bordItemdTmp
										WHERE ISNULL(bordItemId, '0') <> 0 							
										FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))

				/**/
				IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) bordItemId FROM @bordItemdTmp WHERE bordItemId IN(SELECT BuyOrderItemId FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId )),0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @RemoveBord = 1
				END				
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CrsrBordIds,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@RemoveBord,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO @bordIdTmp (bordId)
				SELECT 
					CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrBordIds,',')
				WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
				AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> @BuyOrderId		
				
				SET @CrsrBordIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(bordId, '0') AS NVARCHAR(50))
									FROM  @bordIdTmp
									WHERE ISNULL(bordId, '0') <> 0 							
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))

				
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
			SET [stsupIBuyOrderIds] = @CrsrBordIds
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] = @CrsrBordItemIds
			WHERE [StockSupplyItemId] = @CrsrStockSupplyItemId			
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrUpStsupItem INTO @CrsrStockSupplyItemId,@CrsrBordIds,@CrsrBordItemIds
		END
		CLOSE CrsrUpStsupItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrUpStsupItem
		/*RZ14020925- End*/
		/*---------------------------------------*/		
		DELETE WSBuyOrderItems_Lang  WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemId
		DELETE WSBuyOrderItems  WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemId
		Select 1 as Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	Select 0 as Result
END CATCH
/**/
END







GO

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteWSOrderPayments]





/*RZ960127 For multi language*/
/*AI14020502 - Delete money bag*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDeleteWSOrderPayments]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteWSOrderPayments
*/
@OrderPaymentId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/*AI14020502*/
			DECLARE @ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId INT = (
						SELECT [ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId] 
						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
						WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId)

			DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderPayments_Lang]  WHERE OrderPaymentId = @OrderPaymentId
			DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]  WHERE OrderPaymentId = @OrderPaymentId

			/*AI14020502*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId, 0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyMoneyBag_Lang]  WHERE [CompanyMoneyBagId] = @ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId
				DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyMoneyBag]  WHERE [CompanyMoneyBagId] = @ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId
			END

			SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspInsertDiscountCodeHistory]







/*RZ14011126*/
/*NKR14030224- Add @OrderId*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspInsertDiscountCodeHistory]
(
	/*
		DECLARE @InsertedId INT = 0
		EXEC WSspInsertDiscountCodeHistory 
		@DiscountCodeId = 9,
		@DisCode = '5656565',
		@DisCmpId = NULL,
		@DisPrdId = NULL,
		@PreOrderId = NULL,
		@UserId = 3,
		@FPId = 1,
		@Month1 = 11,
		@RegisterDate = '2023-02-15',
		@RegisterDatePersian = '14011126',
		@AppType = 'dasti', 
		@LanguageID = 1065,
		@InsertedId = @InsertedId OUTPUT

		SELECT @InsertedId
	*/
	@DiscountCodeId INT,
	@DisCode NVARCHAR(50),
	@DisCmpId INT = NULL,
	@DisPrdId INT = NULL,
	@PreOrderId INT = NULL,
	@UserId INT,
	@FPId INT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
	@LanguageID INT,
	@InsertedId INT OUTPUT,
	@OrderId INT = NULL/*NKR14030224*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@DisPrdId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @DisPrdId = (SELECT TOP (1) [DisPrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode] WHERE [DiscountCodeId] = @DiscountCodeId)
	END
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@DisCmpId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @DisCmpId = (SELECT TOP (1) [DisCmpId] FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode] WHERE [DiscountCodeId] = @DiscountCodeId)
	END
	/**/
	/*NKR14030226*/
	IF(ISNULL(@DisCmpId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF (ISNULL(@OrderId, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @DisCmpId = (SELECT O.ordCompanyId FROM WSOrder O WHERE O.OrderID = @OrderId)
			/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@PreOrderId, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @DisCmpId = (SELECT PO.pordCompanyId FROM WSPreOrder PO WHERE PO.PreOrderID = @PreOrderId)
			/**/
		END
		/**/
	END
	/*!NKR14030226*/
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@DisCode,'') = '')
	BEGIN
		SET @DisCode = (SELECT TOP (1) [DisCode] FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode] WHERE [DiscountCodeId] = @DiscountCodeId)
	END
	/*----------------------*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
		([DiscountCodeId]
		,[DisCode]
		,[DisCmpId]
		,[DisPrdId]
		,[FirstRegisterUserId]
		,[FirstRegisterDate]
		,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
		,[UserId]
		,[FPId]
		,[Month1]
		,[RegisterDate]
		,[RegisterDatePersian]		
		,[AppType]
		,[LanguageID]
		,[PreOrderId]
		,[OrderId])
	VALUES
		(@DiscountCodeId
		,@DisCode
		,@DisCmpId
		,@DisPrdId
		,@UserId
		,@RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian
		,@UserId
		,@FPId
		,@Month1
		,@RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian		
		,@AppType
		,@LanguageID
		,@PreOrderId
		,@OrderId)
	SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
/**/
END

@CostSheetId INT,

/*BuyOrder*/
@BuyOrderOprId INT,
@bordOrderNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@bordOrderDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@bordOrderDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@bordReferUserId INT,
@bordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@bordCompanyId INT,
@bordCompanyPersonId INT,
@bordProjectId INT,
@bordReqCmpId INT,
@bordReqPrsId INT,
@bordReqPrjId INT,
@bordFacilityCmpId INT = NULL,
@bordFacilityPrsId INT = NULL,
@bordFacilityPrjId INT = NULL,
@bordDeliveryTerm NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@bordDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
@bordDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@bordSupplierDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
@bordSupplierDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@bordFinalDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
@bordFinalDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,

@bordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@bordNote NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
@bordFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@bordFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,

@bordFreightCurrUnId INT = NULL,

@bordFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@bordCustomsPart NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@bordCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@bordCustomsCurrUnId INT = NULL,
@bordCustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
@bordFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,	
@bordTotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@bordCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@bordCommissionCurrUnId INT = NULL,
@bordCommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
@bordFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,	
@BuyOrderFieldLang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@bordIWHId INT = NULL,		
/*Payment*/
@BuyOrderPaymentJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT,
/**/
@InsertedId INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*=============================== Variables ======================*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT,@bordDay NVARCHAR(50),@bordTime NVARCHAR(50),@bordNumber BIGINT,
@InsertedBordId INT,@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(15),@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(15),
@PrdFirstGroupId INT = NULL,
@PrdSecondGroupId INT = NULL,
@ProductId INT = NULL
/*=============================== Set Variables ==================*/
SET @bordTime = CAST(CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME) AS NVARCHAR(8))
SET @bordDay = DATEPART(dw,@RegisterDate)
SET @RegisterTime = CAST(CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME) AS NVARCHAR(8))
SET @RegisterDay = DATEPART(dw,@RegisterDate)
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0) = 0 )
BEGIN
	SET @bordCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ctshSrcCurrencyUnitId] FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheets] WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId)
END

IF(ISNULL(@bordIWHId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @bordIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 0)
END
/*================================================================*/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/	
BEGIN TRY  
/**/    		
	/*======================= Get bordNumber ==========================*/			
	SET @bordNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnBuyOrderNumber] (@BuyOrderOprId))
	SET @InsertedNumber = @bordNumber
	/*=============================== BuyOrder ==========================*/		
	/*-------------------- TRAN 2 --------------------------*/	
	BEGIN TRAN	
		/*RZ991216- Add bordNumRef*/
		DECLARE @bordNumRef NVARCHAR(150)
		IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmBuyOrderDataEntry' )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actAutoCalcNumRef'
								AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmBuyOrderDataEntry' ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
		BEGIN
		/**/		
		DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE ([bordNumRef] NVARCHAR(150))			
		INSERT INTO @TmpTable([bordnumRef])	
		EXEC dbo.WSspReturnBuyOrderNumRef 
		@BordCompanyId = @bordCompanyId,
		@BordProjectId = @bordProjectId,
		@BordReferUserId = @bordReferUserId,
		@BordNumber = @bordNumber,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID 

		SET @bordNumRef =(SELECT TOP 1 bordNumRef FROM @TmpTable)
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ991216- End*/

		/*RZ991210- Add fields in insert(facility,freight,customs,commission)*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
			([BuyOrderOprId]
			,[bordNumber]
			,[bordOrderNumber]
			,[bordDate]
			,[bordOrderDate]
			,[bordOrderDateTime]
			,[bordCompanyId]
			,[bordCompanyPersonId]
			,[bordProjectId]
			,[bordTax]
			,[bordTaxIndex]
			,[bordCalculateTax]
			,[bordStatusId]
			,[bordIsFixed]
			,[bordImportDate]
			,[UserId]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[bordFirstRegisterUserId]
			,[bordDay]
			,[bordTime]
			,[bordReferUserId]
			,[bordNote]
			,[bordDateTime]
			,[bordImportDateTime]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[bordCurrencyUnitId]
			,[bordFacilityCmpId]
			,[bordFacilityPrsId]
			,[bordFacilityPrjId]
			,[bordReqCmpId]
			,[bordReqPrsId]
			,[bordReqPrjId]
			,[bordDeliveryTerm]
			,[bordDeliveryDate]
			,[bordDeliveryDatePersian]
			,[bordFacilityCost]
			,[bordFacilityCostPercent]
			,[bordSupplierDeliveryDate]
			,[bordSupplierDeliveryDatePersian]
			,[bordForwarderName]
			,[bordFreightCost]
			,[bordFreightCurrUnId]
			,[bordFreightCurrencyRate]
			,[bordFinalFreightCost]
			,[bordCustomsPart]
			,[bordCustomsFee]
			,[bordCustomsCurrUnId]
			,[bordCustomsCurrencyRate]
			,[bordFinalCustomsFee]
			,[bordFinalDeliveryDate]
			,[bordFinalDeliveryDatePersian]
			,[bordTotalFinalCost]
			,[bordCommission]
			,[bordCommissionCurrUnId]
			,[bordCommissionCurrencyRate]
			,[bordFinalCommission]
			,[bordCostSheetId]
			,[bordnumRef]/*RZ991216- Add bordNumRef*/)
		VALUES
			(@BuyOrderOprId
			,@bordNumber
			,@bordOrderNumber
			,@RegisterDatePersian/*bordDate*/
			,@bordOrderDate
			,@bordOrderDateTime
			,@bordCompanyId
			,@bordCompanyPersonId
			,@bordProjectId
			,@bordTax
			,@bordTaxIndex
			,@bordCalculateTax	
			,1 /*bordStatusId*/		
			,1 /*bordIsFixed*/
			,@RegisterDatePersian /*bordImportDate*/
			,@UserId
			,@FPId
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@UserId			
			,@RegisterDay /*bordDay*/
			,@RegisterTime /*bordTime*/
			,@bordReferUserId
			,@bordNote
			,@RegisterDate/*bordDateTime*/
			,@RegisterDate /*bordImportDateTime*/
			,@AppType
			,@LanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordFacilityCmpId
			,@bordFacilityPrsId
			,@bordFacilityPrjId
			,@bordReqCmpId
			,@bordReqPrsId
			,@bordReqPrjId
			,@bordDeliveryTerm
			,@bordDeliveryDate
			,@bordDeliveryDatePersian			
			,@bordFacilityCost
			,@bordFacilityCostPercent
			,@bordSupplierDeliveryDate
			,@bordSupplierDeliveryDatePersian
			,@bordForwarderName
			,@bordFreightCost
			,@bordFreightCurrUnId
			,@bordFreightCurrencyRate
			,@bordFinalFreightCost
			,@bordCustomsPart
			,@bordCustomsFee
			,@bordCustomsCurrUnId
			,@bordCustomsCurrencyRate
			,@bordFinalCustomsFee
			,@bordFinalDeliveryDate
			,@bordFinalDeliveryDatePersian
			,@bordTotalFinalCost
			,@bordCommission
			,@bordCommissionCurrUnId
			,@bordCommissionCurrencyRate
			,@bordFinalCommission
			,@CostSheetId
			,@bordNumRef/*RZ991216- Add bordNumRef*/)		
		SET @InsertedBordId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*Insert records in lang table*/
		IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderFieldLang,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*======================== Create Tmp table for language ==========================*/
			/*Sample= @BuyOrderFieldLang = N'[LangId=1033|,|bordDeliveryTerm=TextI|,|bordNote=bordNote|,|bordForwarderName=bordForwarderName|,|bordCustomsPart=bordCustomsPart|,|]@[LangId=1025|,|bordDeliveryTerm=TextII|,|]@',*/
			DECLARE @BordLangTmpTable TABLE (
				[LangId] INT,
				[bordDeliveryTerm] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				/*RZ991210- Add fields*/				
				[bordNote] NVARCHAR(300),
				[bordForwarderName] NVARCHAR(1000),
				[bordCustomsPart] NVARCHAR(1000)
				/*RZ991210- End*/
			)
			DECLARE @bordLangId INT,@bordDeliveryTermTxt NVARCHAR(MAX),@BordText NVARCHAR(MAX),
			@bordNoteTxt  NVARCHAR(300),@bordForwarderNameTxt NVARCHAR(1000),@bordCustomsPartTxt NVARCHAR(1000)/*RZ991210- Add fields*/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE CrsrBordLang CURSOR LOCAL FOR 
			/**/	
				SELECT SplitData  
				FROM fnSplitString(@BuyOrderFieldLang, '@')
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrBordLang
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrBordLang INTO @BordText
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*================================================================================*/
				DECLARE @BordField NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
				SET @bordLangId = NULL
				SET @bordDeliveryTermTxt = NULL
				/*RZ991210- Add fields*/				
				SET @bordNoteTxt = NULL
				SET @bordForwarderNameTxt = NULL
				SET @bordCustomsPartTxt = NULL
				/*RZ991210- End*/
				/**/
				/*****************************************************************/
				IF(ISNULL(@BordText,'') <> '') 
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @BordText = SUBSTRING(@BordText, 2, LEN(@BordText)-2)			
					/*===================== Get Fields ==========================*/
					WHILE LEN(@BordText) > 0
					BEGIN
						IF PATINDEX('%|,|%', @BordText) > 0
						BEGIN
							SET @BordField = SUBSTRING(@BordText, 0, PATINDEX('%|,|%', @BordText))
							/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
							/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
							/*For example => LangId=1065 => LangId= => LangId => Compare => value*/
							DECLARE @BordFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
							DECLARE @BordFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
							SET @BordFieldName = SUBSTRING(@BordField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@BordField)-1)                                    
							SET @BordFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@BordField, CHARINDEX('=', @BordField)+1, Len(@BordField))
							/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
							/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
							/*BEGIN*/
							IF @BordFieldName = 'LangId'
							BEGIN
								SET @BordLangId = CASE WHEN CAST(ISNULL(@BordFieldValue,'0') AS INT) = 0 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@BordFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @BordFieldName = 'bordDeliveryTerm'
							BEGIN
								SET @bordDeliveryTermTxt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BordFieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @BordFieldValue END
							END						
							/*--------------------------*/								
							/*RZ991210- Add fields*/	
							IF @BordFieldName = 'bordNote'
							BEGIN
								SET @bordNoteTxt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BordFieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @BordFieldValue END
							END						
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @BordFieldName = 'bordForwarderName'
							BEGIN
								SET @bordForwarderNameTxt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BordFieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @BordFieldValue END
							END						
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @BordFieldName = 'bordCustomsPart'
							BEGIN
								SET @bordCustomsPartTxt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BordFieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @BordFieldValue END
							END						
							/*--------------------------*/			
							/*RZ991210- End*/							
							/*--------------------------*/		
							/*--------------------------*/				
							/*END */
							/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
							/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
							SET @BordText = SUBSTRING(@BordText,LEN(@BordField + '|,|') + 1,LEN(@BordText))
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @BordField = @BordText
							SET @BordText = NULL       
						END
					END
					/*===========================================================*/
					INSERT INTO @BordLangTmpTable ([LangId],[bordDeliveryTerm],[bordNote],[bordForwarderName],[bordCustomsPart])
					VALUES(@BordLangId,@bordDeliveryTermTxt,@bordNoteTxt,@bordForwarderNameTxt,@bordCustomsPartTxt)/*RZ991210- Add fields*/
				/**/
				END
				/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrBordLang INTO @BordText
			END
			CLOSE CrsrBordLang
			DEALLOCATE CrsrBordLang
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
			   ([BuyOrderID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[bordDeliveryTerm]
			   /*RZ991210- Add fields*/
			   ,[bordNote]
			   ,[bordForwarderName]
			   ,[bordCustomsPart]
			   /*RZ991210- End*/)
			SELECT
				@InsertedBordId
			   ,[LangId]
			   ,[bordDeliveryTerm]
			   /*RZ991210- Add fields*/
			   ,[bordNote]
			   ,[bordForwarderName]
			   ,[bordCustomsPart]
			   /*RZ991210- End*/
			FROM @BordLangTmpTable
			WHERE [LangId] <> 0
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*===============================================================================*/
		/**/
		END	
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +='Error In Insert BuyOrder' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	
	/*============================= BuyOrderItems ========================*/		
	/*-------------------- TRAN 3 --------------------------*/	
	BEGIN TRAN	
		/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ991210- Add multi product to buyOrder*/
		DECLARE @CostSheetItemId INT,@CostSheetProductId INT,@ctshPrdName NVARCHAR(1000),
		@PrdCode NVARCHAR(50),@ctshPrdGroupId INT,@ctshIPreOrderItemId INT,
		@ctshIQuantity FLOAT,@ctshICost NUMERIC(18,2),@ctshITotalCost NUMERIC(18,2)
		,@ctshPrdId INT/*RZ14020329- Add ctshPrdId*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_Item CURSOR LOCAL FOR	
			SELECT 
				 [CostSheetItemId]								
				,CHI.[CostSheetProductId]
				,CPRD.[ctshPrdName]				
				,[ctshIQuantity]
				,[ctshICost]
				,[ctshITotalCost]					
				,CPRD.[ctshPrdGroupId]	
				,CHI.[ctshIPreOrderItemId]	
				,CPRD.ctshPrdId /*RZ14020329- Add ctshPrdId*/
			FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
			INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
			ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
			WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
			AND CHI.[CostSheetItemId] IN (SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
										  FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CostSheetItemIds,','))  			
			ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]
					
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Item
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @CostSheetItemId,@CostSheetProductId,@ctshPrdName,
										   @ctshIQuantity,@ctshICost,@ctshITotalCost,										   
										   @ctshPrdGroupId,@ctshIPreOrderItemId,@ctshPrdId /*RZ14020329- Add ctshPrdId*/
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @bordIOrderView INT = NULL,	@bordIPrdCostGroupId INT = NULL,@BuyOrderItemId INT = NULL
			/*----------------- Product ------------------*/
			SET @PrdFirstGroupId = NULL
			SET @PrdSecondGroupId = NULL
			SET @ProductId = NULL
			SET @PrdCode = NULL	
			SET @ProductId = NULL	
			SET @BuyOrderItemId = NULL		 
			/**/
			/*SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 P.ProductID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P 
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.PrdName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) =
										REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ctshPrdName AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
										NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
								  AND PrdGroupID = @ctshPrdGroupId				  
								 )*/


			/*RZ14020329- Add ctshPrdId*/
			SET @ProductId = @ctshPrdId
			IF(ISNULL(@ProductId,0) =  0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 P.ProductID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P 
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.PrdName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) =
										REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ctshPrdName AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
										NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
								  AND PrdGroupID = @ctshPrdGroupId				  
								 )
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14020329- End*/

			IF(ISNULL(@ProductId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*SET groups and prdcode*/
				/*IF(ISNULL(@PrdFirstGroupId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @PrdFirstGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PrdFirstGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup])
				END
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdSecondGroupId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @PrdSecondGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PrdSecondGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE [FirstGroupId] = @PrdFirstGroupId)
				END	*/	
					
				/*RZ991210- Add multi product to buyOrder*/					
				SET @PrdSecondGroupId = @ctshPrdGroupId
				SET @PrdFirstGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FirstGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupID = @PrdSecondGroupId)
				/*!RZ991210*/						
				/**/
				SET @PrdCode = (SELECT dbo.WSfnMakeAndReturnPrdCode (@PrdFirstGroupId, @PrdSecondGroupId))
				/*SET @ctshPrdName = (SELECT TOP 1 [ctshPrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] WHERE [CostSheetProductId] = @CostSheetProductId)*/
				/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSProducts]
					([PrdGroupID]
					,[PrdCode]
					,[PrdName]					
					,[UserId]
					,[Month1]
					,[FPId]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]					
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate])
				VALUES
					(@PrdSecondGroupId
					,@PrdCode
					,@ctshPrdName					
					,@UserId
					,@Month1
					,@FPId
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian					
					,@AppType
					,@LanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate)

				SET @ProductId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
				/*Insert records in lang table*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
					([ProductID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[PrdName])
				SELECT
						@ProductId
					,[LanguageID]
					,[ctshPrdName]
				FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts_Lang] 
				WHERE [CostSheetProductId] = @CostSheetProductId	
				/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END

			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			SET @bordIPrdCostGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductCostGroupId 
										FROM dbo.WSProductCost PP
										WHERE PP.PrdId = @ProductId)
			IF(ISNULL(@bordIPrdCostGroupId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @bordIPrdCostGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductCostGroupId 
											FROM dbo.WSProductCostGroup PP
											ORDER BY PP.OrderView)																						
			/**/
			END
			/**/				
			DECLARE @ItemCount INT = NULL
			SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.BuyOrderId = @InsertedBordId)
			SET @bordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@ctshIPreOrderItemId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ctshIPreOrderItemId = NULL
			END			
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------ Items -------------------*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
				([BuyOrderId]
				,[bordIPrdId]
				,[bordIQuantity]
				,[bordICost]
				,[bordITotalCost]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[bordICurrencyUnitId]
				,[bordIOrderView]
				,[bordIWHId]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[bordIPrdCostGroupID]
				,[bordICostSum]	
				/*,[bordIPreOrderItemId]	*/ /*RZ14000117- Not save pordItemId in bordItem*/
				,[bordICostSheetItemId])
			VALUES
				(@InsertedBordId
				,@ProductId
				,@ctshIQuantity
				,@ctshICost
				,@ctshITotalCost
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@bordCurrencyUnitId
				,@bordIOrderView
				,@bordIWHId
				,@AppType
				,@LanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@bordIPrdCostGroupID
				,@ctshITotalCost
				/*,@ctshIPreOrderItemId*//*RZ14000117- Not save pordItemId in bordItem*/
				,@CostSheetItemId)
			SET @BuyOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]
				   ([BuyOrderItemId]
				   ,[LanguageID])
				SELECT
					@BuyOrderItemId
				   ,L.[LanguageID]				   
				FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
				WHERE LanguageID <> @LanguageID
				AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1				
			/*------------------ CostSheet Item -----------*/
			/*RZ14000117- Not save buyorder in costsheet*/
			/*UPDATE [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems]
			SET [ctshIBuyOrderId] = @InsertedBordId,
				[ctshIBuyOrderItemId] = @BuyOrderItemId
			WHERE [CostSheetItemId] = @CostSheetItemId*/			
			/*============================================================================*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @CostSheetItemId,@CostSheetProductId,@ctshPrdName,
											   @ctshIQuantity,@ctshICost,@ctshITotalCost,										   
											   @ctshPrdGroupId,@ctshIPreOrderItemId,@ctshPrdId /*RZ14020329- Add ctshPrdId*/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ991210- End*/		
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/	
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert BuyOrderItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	
	/*=========================== PreOrderPayments ======================*/		
	/*-------------------- TRAN 4 --------------------------*/	
	IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderPaymentJson,'') <> '' AND  ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*------------------ Variables --------------------*/
		DECLARE @bordpayImportDate NVARCHAR(8),
		@bordpayImportDateTime DATETIME,@bordpayTypeId INT,
		@bordpayAmount NUMERIC(18, 2),@OrdPay NVARCHAR(MAX)		
		/*-------------------------------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrPay CURSOR LOCAL FOR 		
				SELECT 
				(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
									FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@BuyOrderPaymentJson) PJIN
									WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@BuyOrderPaymentJson) PJ
				WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
			OPEN CrsrPay
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPay INTO @OrdPay
			/**/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN		
			/**/
				DECLARE @InsertedBordPayId INT = NULL
				/**/				
				SET @bordpayImportDate = NULL
				SET @bordpayImportDateTime = NULL
				SET @bordpayTypeId = NULL
				SET @bordpayAmount = NULL
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdPay,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @OrdPay = @OrdPay + '|'/*for last field*/
					DECLARE @PayField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					WHILE LEN(@OrdPay) > 0
					BEGIN
						IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@OrdPay) > 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @PayField = SUBSTRING(@OrdPay, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @OrdPay))						
							/**/
							DECLARE @PayFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
							DECLARE @PayFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
							SET @PayFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PayField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PayField)-1)                                    
							SET @PayFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PayField, CHARINDEX('=', @PayField)+1, Len(@PayField))
							/**/
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'bordpayTypeId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @bordpayTypeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'bordpayImportDateTime'
							BEGIN						
								SELECT @bordpayImportDateTime = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS DATETIME) END
								/**/
								IF(@bordpayImportDateTime IS NULL)
									SET @bordpayImportDateTime = @RegisterDate							
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'bordpayImportDate'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @bordpayImportDate = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PayFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'bordpayAmount'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @bordpayAmount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/																				
							/*--------------------------*/
							SET @OrdPay = SUBSTRING(@OrdPay,
									  LEN(@PayField + '|') + 1,
									  LEN(@OrdPay))
							/*--------------------------*/								
							/*--------------------------*/
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @PayField = @OrdPay
							SET @OrdPay = NULL						
						END
					END
					/**/	
					/**********************************************************/					
					IF(@bordpayImportDateTime IS NULL)
						SET @bordpayImportDateTime = @RegisterDate
					/**/
					IF(@bordpayImportDateTime IS NOT NULL AND @bordpayImportDate IS NULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @bordpayImportDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@bordpayImportDateTime AS DATE)))						
					END
					/*========================================================*/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderPayments]
						([ImportDate]
						,[bordpayTypeId]
						,[bordpayOrderId]
						,[bordpayAmount]
						,[bordpayDocDate]
						,[bordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
						,[bordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
						,[bordpayFirstRegisteDate]
						,[UserId]
						,[FPId]
						,[Month1]
						,[RegisterDate]
						,[RegisterDatePersian]
						,[ImportDateTime]
						,[bordpayDocDateTime]
						,[AppType]
						,[bordpayDocAmount]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						,[FirstRegisterDate])
					VALUES
						(@bordpayImportDate
						,@bordpayTypeId
						,@InsertedBordId
						,@bordpayAmount
						,@bordpayImportDate
						,@UserId
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDate
						,@UserId
						,@FPId
						,@Month1
						,@RegisterDate
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@bordpayImportDateTime
						,@bordpayImportDateTime
						,@AppType
						,@bordpayAmount
						,@LanguageID
						,@UserId
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDate)
					SET @InsertedBordPayId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
					/*Insert records in lang table*/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderPayments_Lang]
						([BuyOrderPaymentId]
						,[LanguageID])
					SELECT
						 @InsertedBordPayId
						,[LanguageID]				
					FROM [dbo].[Languages]
					WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
					AND [LanguageID] <> @LanguageID					
					/*========================================================*/		
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPay INTO @OrdPay
			END
			/**/
			CLOSE CrsrPay
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPay
			/**/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage='Error In Insert BuyOrderPayments' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*=========================== Calculate BuyOrder ====================*/		
	EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts] @InsertedBordId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @LanguageID
	/*RZ991210- Change sp*/
	DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @bordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	SELECT  @SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([bordICostSum],0)  + ISNULL([bordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)		
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
	WHERE BuyOrderId = @InsertedBordId
	AND ISNULL(bordINotInSum, 0) = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@bordTotalAmount = [bordTotalAmount]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
		WHERE [BuyOrderID] = @InsertedBordId
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@bordFacilityCostPercent,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @bordFacilityCost = (@SumPriceAmount * @bordFacilityCostPercent) / 100;
			/**/
			END

			SET @bordTotalFinalCost = ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordFacilityCost,0) 
									+ ISNULL(@bordFinalFreightCost,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalCustomsFee,0) 
									+ ISNULL(@bordFinalCommission,0)
									
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]		
			SET bordTotalFinalCost = @bordTotalFinalCost,
				bordFacilityCost = @bordFacilityCost
			WHERE [BuyOrderID] = @InsertedBordId
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ991211- End*/				
	/*=========================================================================*/		
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SET @InsertedId = @InsertedBordId
		SET @InsertedNumber = @bordNumber
		SELECT 1					
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @InsertedId = 0
		SET @InsertedNumber = 0				 
		SELECT 0			
	/**/
	END	
END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	/*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
    SET @InsertedId = 0
	SET @InsertedNumber = 0		
	SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	

/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[WSspIssueBuyOrderFromStockSupply]




/*RZ14020918*/
/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
/*RZ14021128- Add bordReqCmpId*/
/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspIssueBuyOrderFromStockSupply]	
(		
/*
	declare @InsertedBuyOrderId INT = 0, @InsertedBordNumber INT = 0
	exec WSspIssueBuyOrderFromStockSupply  	
	@StockSupplyId = 24,
	@BordDate = '14020918',
	@BordDateTime = '2023-12-09',
	@StsupItemList = NULL,
	@BordCompanyId = 6156,
	@BordCompanyPersonId = 17997,
	@BordProjectId = 6900,
	@BuyOrderDefaultSettingId = NULL,	
	@UserId = 3,
	@Month1 = 9,
	@FPId = 30,
	@RegisterDate = '2023-12-09',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14020918',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@LanguageID = 1033,
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,
	@InsertedBuyOrderId = @InsertedBuyOrderId OUTPUT,
	@InsertedBordNumber = @InsertedBordNumber OUTPUT
	
	select @InsertedBuyOrderId,@InsertedBordNumber
*/
@StockSupplyId INT,
@BordDate NVARCHAR(8),
@BordDateTime DATETIME,
@StsupItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@BordCompanyId INT,
@BordCompanyPersonId INT,
@BordProjectId INT,
@BuyOrderDefaultSettingId INT = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@LanguageID INT,
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
@InsertedBuyOrderId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedBordNumber BIGINT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------------- Set PordItemList -------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/		
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [BuyOrderId]		  			
			   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
			   WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.[value],'0') AS INT)
										 FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
										 CROSS APPLY ((SELECT [value] FROM STRING_SPLIT([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems
										 WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId 
										 AND ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'') <>'')),0) = 0) 
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @StsupItemList  = (SELECT 
									StockSupplyItemId [Id],			
									stsupIQuantity [Value]			
								FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
								WHERE StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId								
								AND stsupIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
														FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
														WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )							
								ORDER BY ISNULL(stsupIOrderView, StockSupplyItemId)	
								FOR JSON PATH)															   								 						

	/**/
	END
	ELSE /*IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [StockSupplyId] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] WHERE [stsupPreOrderId] = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0)*/ /*PreOrder have stockSupply*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @StsupItemList  = (SELECT 
									StockSupplyItemId [Id],		
									(ISNULL(stsupIQuantity,0) - 
									 ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0)) 
											FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI
											WHERE  bordIPrdId = stsupIPrdId
											AND [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.[value],'0') AS INT)
																	FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
																	CROSS APPLY ((SELECT [value] FROM STRING_SPLIT([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems
																	WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId 
																	AND ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'') <>'')
											),0)	
									) [Value]																	   			
								FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
								WHERE StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId								
								AND stsupIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
														FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
														WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )							
								ORDER BY ISNULL(stsupIOrderView, StockSupplyItemId)	
								FOR JSON PATH)		
	/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

	SET @FinalResult = -1

/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
,@bordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,@bordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL

DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
BEGIN TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup

IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'') <>'')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*--------------------------------------------*/			
	SET @BuyOrderId = NULL
	SET @InsertedBuyOrderId = NULL
	SET @bordNumber = NULL

			,@UserId

			,@bordDeliveryTerm
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordDefaultSettingId
			,@bordVerificationWorkFlowId
			/*RZ14021128- Add bordReqCmpId*/
			,@bordReqCmpId

			/*RZ14021128- End*/
			/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
			,@bordCurrencyUnitIdI
			,@bordCurrencyUnitIRate
			/*RZ14030305- End*/
			)
								
		SELECT @BuyOrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@bordDefaultSettingId,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
					([BuyOrderID]

				SELECT
					@BuyOrderId
					,[LanguageID]

					,[Subject]						
					,ISNULL(CAST((SELECT TOP(1) [stsupNote] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply_Lang] STLN WHERE STLN.StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId AND STLN.LanguageID = BS.LanguageID) AS NVARCHAR(300)) ,[Note])						
					,[DeliveryTerm]
				FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderDefaultSetting_Lang] BS
				WHERE [BuyOrderDefaultSettingId] = @bordDefaultSettingId
				AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID				
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@bordDefaultSettingId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
					([BuyOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[bordNote])
				SELECT
					@BuyOrderId
					,[LanguageID]
					,CAST([stsupNote] AS NVARCHAR(300))
				FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply_Lang] STLN
				WHERE STLN.StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId
				AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID				
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSBuyOrder' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	SET @InsertedBuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @StockSupplyItemId INT = NULL,@stsupIPrdId INT = NULL,
		@stsupIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
		@stsupIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@stsupIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
		@stsupIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIWHId INT = NULL,
		@stsupITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
		@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIPrdColorId INT = NULL,
		@stsupIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
		@TmpI FLOAT = NULL, @PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
		@stsupIBuyOrderIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*---------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE CrsrStsupBordItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/
			SELECT 
				 [StockSupplyItemId]					
				,[stsupIPrdId]
				,[stsupIQuantity]
				,[stsupIDescription]
				,[stsupIQuantityDesc]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitId]
				,[stsupIFCR]
				,[stsupIFCRI]
				,[stsupIWHId]
				,[stsupITracing]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitIdI]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[stsupIPrdColorId]
				,[stsupIPrdNickname]					
				,[stsupILinkAccWHIdStr]	
				,[stsupIBuyOrderIds]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds]
			FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] STI
			WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId
			AND StockSupplyItemId IN(SELECT [Id]
									FROM OPENJSON(@StsupItemList )
									WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
									) AS JS	
								)					
			AND stsupIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
									WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )				
			ORDER BY ISNULL(stsupIOrderView, StockSupplyItemId)	

		/**/
		OPEN CrsrStsupBordItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrStsupBordItem INTO	 @StockSupplyItemId,@stsupIPrdId,@stsupIQuantity
												,@stsupIDescription,@stsupIQuantityDesc,@stsupIPrdUnitId
												,@stsupIFCR,@stsupIFCRI,@stsupIWHId
												,@stsupITracing,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@stsupIPrdColorId,@stsupIPrdNickname,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr	
												,@stsupIBuyOrderIds,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- WSBuyOrderItems ------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/

			DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
			SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
							FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'[]'))
							WITH (
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
							) AS JS	
							WHERE [Id] = @StockSupplyItemId)
			IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/	
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrStsupBordItem INTO	 @StockSupplyItemId,@stsupIPrdId,@stsupIQuantity
												,@stsupIDescription,@stsupIQuantityDesc,@stsupIPrdUnitId
												,@stsupIFCR,@stsupIFCRI,@stsupIWHId
												,@stsupITracing,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@stsupIPrdColorId,@stsupIPrdNickname,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr	
												,@stsupIBuyOrderIds,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
				CONTINUE
			/**/
			END
			ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
			BEGIN
			/**/					
				SET @stsupIQuantity = @CurrQty
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/								
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
			SET @TmpI = NULL
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @stsupIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@stsupIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@stsupIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@stsupIFCRI,1)
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@stsupIQuantity,1) 					
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@stsupIQuantity,1)

				IF(ISNULL(@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END	
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
			DECLARE @bordICost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordITotalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@bordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordICostSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordIPrdCostGroupID INT = NULL
			/**/					
			SELECT TOP(1) 
				@bordICost = [Cost],
				@bordIPrdCostGroupID = [CostGroupId]
			FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'[]'))
			WITH (
				[Id] INT '$.Id',
				[Cost] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.Cost',
				[CostGroupId] INT '$.CostGroupId'	
			) AS JS	
			WHERE [Id] = @StockSupplyItemId

			IF(ISNULL(@bordIPrdCostGroupID,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @bordIPrdCostGroupID = @PrdCostGroupID
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------*/
			SET @bordICostSum = ISNULL(@bordICost,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@CalculateTaxItem,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@TaxIndexItem,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpI,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @bordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) * ISNULL(@TaxIndexItem,0))/100
					SET @bordITax = ISNULL(@bordITaxSum,0) / ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END	
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CalculateTaxItem,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TaxIndexItem = NULL
			/**/
			END	
			SET @bordITotalCost = ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) + ISNULL(@bordITaxSum,0)
			/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN				
				SET @bordICost = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordICost))
				SET @bordITotalCost = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITotalCost))			
				SET @bordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITax))
				SET @bordICostSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordICostSum))
				SET @bordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITaxSum))
			END
			/*RZ14021124- End*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @BuyOrderItemId INT = 0,@bordIOrderView INT = 0,@ItemCount INT = NULL
			SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI WHERE BOI.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
			SET @bordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
				([BuyOrderId]
				,[bordIPrdId]
				,[bordIQuantity]
				,[bordICost]
				,[bordITotalCost]
				,[bordIDescription]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[bordIQuantityDesc]
				,[bordIPrdUnitId]
				,[bordICurrencyUnitId]
				,[bordIOrderView]
				,[bordIFCR]
				,[bordITax]
				,[bordITaxIndex]
				,[bordICalculateTax]
				,[bordIFCRI]
				,[bordIWHId]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[bordIPrdCostGroupID]
				,[bordITracing]
				,[bordICostSum]
				,[bordITaxSum]					
				,[bordILinkAccWHIdStr]
				,[bordIPrdUnitIdI]
				,[bordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[bordIPrdColorId]
				,[bordIPrdNickname])
			VALUES
				(@BuyOrderId
				,@stsupIPrdId
				,@stsupIQuantity
				,@bordICost/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@bordITotalCost/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@stsupIDescription
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@stsupIQuantityDesc
				,@stsupIPrdUnitId
				,@bordCurrencyUnitId
				,@bordIOrderView
				,@stsupIFCR
				,@bordITax/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@TaxIndexItem
				,@CalculateTaxItem 
				,@stsupIFCRI
				,@stsupIWHId
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@bordIPrdCostGroupID
				,@stsupITracing
				,@bordICostSum/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@bordITaxSum	/*RZ14021124- Add price*/				
				,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr
				,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI
				,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
				,@stsupIPrdColorId
				,@stsupIPrdNickname)
														   					 				  
			SELECT @BuyOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderItemId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]
					([BuyOrderItemId]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[bordIDescription]
					,[bordIQuantityDesc]
					,[bordIPrdNickname])
				SELECT
					@BuyOrderItemId
					,STILN.LanguageID
					,stsupIDescription
					,stsupIQuantityDesc
					,stsupIPrdNickname
				FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang] STILN 
				WHERE  STILN.StockSupplyItemId = @StockSupplyItemId
				AND STILN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
				/**/
				/*---------------------- Update StocksupplyItem --------*/					
				DECLARE @AddBordId BIT = 1
				IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderIds,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
						        FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@stsupIBuyOrderIds,',') 
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
								AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) = @BuyOrderId),0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @AddBordId = 0
					END
				/**/
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@AddBordId,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderIds,'') <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @stsupIBuyOrderIds = @stsupIBuyOrderIds + ','
					END
					SET @stsupIBuyOrderIds =  ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderIds,'') + CAST(@BuyOrderId AS NVARCHAR(50))
				/**/
				END
				/*--------*/
				DECLARE @AddBordIId BIT = 1
				IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
						        FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,',') 
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
								AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) = @BuyOrderItemId),0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @AddBordIId = 0
					END
				/**/
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@AddBordIId,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,'') <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds = @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds + ','
					END
					SET @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds = ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,'') + CAST(@BuyOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(50))
				/**/
				END
				/*--------*/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
				SET [stsupIBuyOrderIds] = @stsupIBuyOrderIds
					,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] = @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
				WHERE StockSupplyItemId = @StockSupplyItemId
				/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrStsupBordItem INTO	 @StockSupplyItemId,@stsupIPrdId,@stsupIQuantity
												,@stsupIDescription,@stsupIQuantityDesc,@stsupIPrdUnitId
												,@stsupIFCR,@stsupIFCRI,@stsupIWHId
												,@stsupITracing,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@stsupIPrdColorId,@stsupIPrdNickname,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr	
												,@stsupIBuyOrderIds,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
		END
		CLOSE CrsrStsupBordItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrStsupBordItem
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSBuyOrderItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts]
		@BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId,
		@Month1 = @Month1,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID			
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/		 
	/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END		
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup	
		SET @InsertedBuyOrderId = 0
		SET @InsertedBordNumber = 0	
		SET @FinalResult = 0	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup
		SET @FinalResult = 1	
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup	
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @FinalResult AS 'Result'
/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END













GO

@BordDate NVARCHAR(8),
@BordDateTime DATETIME,
@StsupItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@BordCompanyId INT,
@BordCompanyPersonId INT,
@BordProjectId INT,
@BuyOrderDefaultSettingId INT = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@LanguageID INT,
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
