@InsertedBordNumber BIGINT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------------- Set PordItemList -------------------------------*/

DECLARE @FinalResult INT = NULL,@StockSupplyItemIds NVARCHAR(4000)

IF(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @FinalResult = -2		
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @StockSupplyItemIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(JS.[Id] , 0) AS NVARCHAR(50))  		
								FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'[]'))
								WITH(
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'							  
								) JS
								WHERE ISNULL(JS.[Value],0) > 0							
								FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))	
/**/
END
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/

/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*Check preorder have stockSupply*/
DECLARE @TmpCheckStsupHaveBuyOrder TABLE(
		[Result] INT
)

INSERT INTO @TmpCheckStsupHaveBuyOrder ([Result])
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrderByItemIds] @StockSupplyItemIds = @StockSupplyItemIds

IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1)[Result] FROM @TmpCheckStsupHaveBuyOrder),0) = 1)
BEGIN
	SET @FinalResult = -1	
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @BuyOrderId INT = NULL,@BuyOrderOprId INT = NULL,
@bordTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@bordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
@bordSubject NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@bordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@bordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,@bordPrintFormatId INT = NULL,@bordSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
@bordDay NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@bordTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@bordNote NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
@bordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@bordDeliveryTerm NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@bordVerificationWorkFlowId INT = NULL,@bordDefaultSettingId INT = NULL,
@TaxIndexItem NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@CalculateTaxItem BIT = NULL,@PrdCostGroupID INT = NULL
/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
,@bordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,@bordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL
/*RZ14030305- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SET @bordTime = CAST(CAST(@BordDateTime AS TIME) AS NVARCHAR(8))
SET @bordDay = DATEPART(dw,@BordDateTime)

SET @PrdCostGroupID = (SELECT TOP(1)[ProductCostGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductCostGroup] PCG ORDER BY OrderView)/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderDefaultSettingId,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT TOP (1) 
		 @bordDefaultSettingId = [BuyOrderDefaultSettingId]
		,@BuyOrderOprId = [BuyOrderOprId]
		,@bordTitle = [Title]
		,@bordSubject = [Subject]
		,@bordTaxIndex = [TaxIndex]
		,@bordCalculateTax = [CalculateTax]
		,@TaxIndexItem = [TaxIndexItem]
		,@CalculateTaxItem = [CalculateTaxItem]
		,@bordPrintFormatId = [PrintFormatId]
		,@bordNote = [Note]
		,@bordCurrencyUnitId = [CurrencyUnitId]
		,@bordDeliveryTerm = [DeliveryTerm]
		,@bordVerificationWorkFlowId = [VerificationWorkFlowId]		
		,@bordCurrencyUnitIdI = [CurrencyUnitIdI]/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderDefaultSetting]
	WHERE [BuyOrderDefaultSettingId] = @BuyOrderDefaultSettingId
/**/
END
/**/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @bordCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
								  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
										= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								  )
/**/
END

DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL
/**/
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @bordCurrencyUnitId)

/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
IF(ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @bordCurrencyUnitIRate = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyRate]
									FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnitRate] CUR
									WHERE CUR.[DestCurrencyUnitId] = @bordCurrencyUnitIdI
									AND CUR.[SrcCurrencyUnitId] = @bordCurrencyUnitId
									ORDER BY [RateDate] DESC)
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*RZ14030305- End*/
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderOprId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @BuyOrderOprId = (SELECT TOP (1) [BuyOrderOprID] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderOpr])
/**/
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
BEGIN TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/	
IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'') <>'')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*--------------------------------------------*/			
	SET @BuyOrderId = NULL
	SET @InsertedBuyOrderId = NULL
	SET @bordNumber = NULL
	SET @InsertedBordNumber = NULL
	/*---------------- StockSupply Number ----------------*/
	SELECT @bordNumber =  (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnBuyOrderNumber](@BuyOrderOprId))
	SET @InsertedBordNumber = @bordNumber
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------- tran1 ----------------*/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/*--------------------------------------------*/	
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
			([BuyOrderOprId]
			,[bordTitle]
			,[bordNumber]
			,[bordDate]
			,[bordCompanyId]
			,[bordCompanyPersonId]
			,[bordProjectId]
			,[bordSubject]
			,[bordTaxIndex]
			,[bordCalculateTax]
			,[bordStatusId]
			,[bordIsFixed]
			,[bordPrintFormatId]
			,[bordImportDate]
			,[UserId]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[bordFirstRegisterUserId]
			,[bordSettlementTypeId]
			,[bordDay]
			,[bordTime]
			,[bordReferUserId]
			,[bordNote]
			,[bordDateTime]
			,[bordImportDateTime]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[bordCurrencyUnitId]
			,[bordDeliveryTerm]
			,[bordFreightCurrUnId]
			,[bordCustomsCurrUnId]
			,[bordCommissionCurrUnId]
			,[bordDefaultSettingId]
			,[bordVerificationWorkFlowId]
			/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
			,[bordCurrencyUnitIdI]
			,[bordCurrencyUnitIRate]
			/*RZ14030305- End*/
			)
		VALUES
			(@BuyOrderOprId
			,@bordTitle
			,@bordNumber
			,@bordDate
			,@bordCompanyId
			,@bordCompanyPersonId
			,@bordProjectId
			,@bordSubject
			,@bordTaxIndex
			,@bordCalculateTax
			,1/*bordStatusId*/
			,1/*bordIsFixed*/
			,@bordPrintFormatId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@UserId
			,@FPId
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@UserId
			,@bordSettlementTypeId
			,@bordDay
			,@bordTime
			,@UserId
			,@bordNote
			,@bordDateTime
			,@RegisterDate
			,@AppType
			,@DefaultLanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordDeliveryTerm
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordCurrencyUnitId
			,@bordDefaultSettingId
			,@bordVerificationWorkFlowId
			/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
			,@bordCurrencyUnitIdI
			,@bordCurrencyUnitIRate
			/*RZ14030305- End*/
			)
								
		SELECT @BuyOrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@bordDefaultSettingId,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
					([BuyOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[bordTitle]
					,[bordSubject]
					,[bordNote]
					,[bordDeliveryTerm])
				SELECT
					@BuyOrderId
					,[LanguageID]
					,[Title]
					,[Subject]						
					,[Note]						
					,[DeliveryTerm]
				FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderDefaultSetting_Lang] BS
				WHERE [BuyOrderDefaultSettingId] = @bordDefaultSettingId
				AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID				
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@bordDefaultSettingId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
					([BuyOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[bordNote])
				SELECT
					@BuyOrderId
					,[LanguageID]
					,NULL
				FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
				WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
				AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID				
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSBuyOrder' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	SET @InsertedBuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @StockSupplyItemId INT = NULL,@stsupIPrdId INT = NULL,
		@stsupIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
		@stsupIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@stsupIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
		@stsupIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIWHId INT = NULL,
		@stsupITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
		@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,@stsupIPrdColorId INT = NULL,
		@stsupIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
		@TmpI FLOAT = NULL, @PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
		@stsupIBuyOrderIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*---------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE CrsrStsupBordItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/
			SELECT 
				 [StockSupplyItemId]					
				,[stsupIPrdId]
				,[stsupIQuantity]
				,[stsupIDescription]
				,[stsupIQuantityDesc]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitId]
				,[stsupIFCR]
				,[stsupIFCRI]
				,[stsupIWHId]
				,[stsupITracing]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitIdI]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[stsupIPrdColorId]
				,[stsupIPrdNickname]					
				,[stsupILinkAccWHIdStr]	
				,[stsupIBuyOrderIds]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds]
			FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] STI
			WHERE StockSupplyItemId IN(SELECT [Id]
									FROM OPENJSON(@StsupItemList )
									WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
									) AS JS	
								)					
			AND stsupIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
									WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )				
			ORDER BY (SELECT TOP(1) [stsupDateTime] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST WHERE ST.StockSupplyId = STI.StockSupplyId) DESC, 
					(SELECT TOP(1) [stsupNumber] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST WHERE ST.StockSupplyId = STI.StockSupplyId)  DESC,
					[stsupIPrdId]

		/**/
		OPEN CrsrStsupBordItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrStsupBordItem INTO	 @StockSupplyItemId,@stsupIPrdId,@stsupIQuantity
												,@stsupIDescription,@stsupIQuantityDesc,@stsupIPrdUnitId
												,@stsupIFCR,@stsupIFCRI,@stsupIWHId
												,@stsupITracing,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@stsupIPrdColorId,@stsupIPrdNickname,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr	
												,@stsupIBuyOrderIds,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- WSBuyOrderItems ------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/

			DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
			SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
							FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'[]'))
							WITH (
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
							) AS JS	
							WHERE [Id] = @StockSupplyItemId)
			IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/	
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrStsupBordItem INTO	 @StockSupplyItemId,@stsupIPrdId,@stsupIQuantity
												,@stsupIDescription,@stsupIQuantityDesc,@stsupIPrdUnitId
												,@stsupIFCR,@stsupIFCRI,@stsupIWHId
												,@stsupITracing,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@stsupIPrdColorId,@stsupIPrdNickname,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr	
												,@stsupIBuyOrderIds,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
				CONTINUE
			/**/
			END
			ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
			BEGIN
			/**/					
				SET @stsupIQuantity = @CurrQty
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/								
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
			SET @TmpI = NULL
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @stsupIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@stsupIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@stsupIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@stsupIFCRI,1)
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@stsupIQuantity,1) 					
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@stsupIQuantity,1)

				IF(ISNULL(@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END	
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
			DECLARE @bordICost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordITotalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@bordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordICostSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordIPrdCostGroupID INT = NULL
			/**/					
			SELECT TOP(1) 
				@bordICost = [Cost],
				@bordIPrdCostGroupID = [CostGroupId]
			FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@StsupItemList ,'[]'))
			WITH (
				[Id] INT '$.Id',
				[Cost] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.Cost',
				[CostGroupId] INT '$.CostGroupId'	
			) AS JS	
			WHERE [Id] = @StockSupplyItemId

			IF(ISNULL(@bordIPrdCostGroupID,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @bordIPrdCostGroupID = @PrdCostGroupID
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------*/
			SET @bordICostSum = ISNULL(@bordICost,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@CalculateTaxItem,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@TaxIndexItem,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpI,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @bordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) * ISNULL(@TaxIndexItem,0))/100
					SET @bordITax = ISNULL(@bordITaxSum,0) / ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END	
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CalculateTaxItem,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TaxIndexItem = NULL
			/**/
			END	
			SET @bordITotalCost = ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) + ISNULL(@bordITaxSum,0)
			/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN				
				SET @bordICost = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordICost))
				SET @bordITotalCost = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITotalCost))			
				SET @bordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITax))
				SET @bordICostSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordICostSum))
				SET @bordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITaxSum))
			END
			/*RZ14021124- End*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @BuyOrderItemId INT = 0,@bordIOrderView INT = 0,@ItemCount INT = NULL
			SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI WHERE BOI.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
			SET @bordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
				([BuyOrderId]
				,[bordIPrdId]
				,[bordIQuantity]
				,[bordICost]
				,[bordITotalCost]
				,[bordIDescription]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[bordIQuantityDesc]
				,[bordIPrdUnitId]
				,[bordICurrencyUnitId]
				,[bordIOrderView]
				,[bordIFCR]
				,[bordITax]
				,[bordITaxIndex]
				,[bordICalculateTax]
				,[bordIFCRI]
				,[bordIWHId]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[bordIPrdCostGroupID]
				,[bordITracing]
				,[bordICostSum]
				,[bordITaxSum]					
				,[bordILinkAccWHIdStr]
				,[bordIPrdUnitIdI]
				,[bordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[bordIPrdColorId]
				,[bordIPrdNickname])
			VALUES
				(@BuyOrderId
				,@stsupIPrdId
				,@stsupIQuantity
				,@bordICost/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@bordITotalCost/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@stsupIDescription
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@stsupIQuantityDesc
				,@stsupIPrdUnitId
				,@bordCurrencyUnitId
				,@bordIOrderView
				,@stsupIFCR
				,@bordITax/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@TaxIndexItem
				,@CalculateTaxItem 
				,@stsupIFCRI
				,@stsupIWHId
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@bordIPrdCostGroupID
				,@stsupITracing
				,@bordICostSum/*RZ14021124- Add price*/
				,@bordITaxSum	/*RZ14021124- Add price*/				
				,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr
				,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI
				,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
				,@stsupIPrdColorId
				,@stsupIPrdNickname)
														   					 				  
			SELECT @BuyOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderItemId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]
					([BuyOrderItemId]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[bordIDescription]
					,[bordIQuantityDesc]
					,[bordIPrdNickname])
				SELECT
					@BuyOrderItemId
					,STILN.LanguageID
					,stsupIDescription
					,stsupIQuantityDesc
					,stsupIPrdNickname
				FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang] STILN 
				WHERE  STILN.StockSupplyItemId = @StockSupplyItemId
				AND STILN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
				/**/
				/*---------------------- Update StocksupplyItem --------*/					
				DECLARE @AddBordId BIT = 1
				IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderIds,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
						        FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@stsupIBuyOrderIds,',') 
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
								AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) = @BuyOrderId),0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @AddBordId = 0
					END
				/**/
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@AddBordId,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderIds,'') <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @stsupIBuyOrderIds = @stsupIBuyOrderIds + ','
					END
					SET @stsupIBuyOrderIds =  ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderIds,'') + CAST(@BuyOrderId AS NVARCHAR(50))
				/**/
				END
				/*--------*/
				DECLARE @AddBordIId BIT = 1
				IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
						        FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,',') 
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
								AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) = @BuyOrderItemId),0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @AddBordIId = 0
					END
				/**/
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@AddBordIId,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,'') <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds = @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds + ','
					END
					SET @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds = ISNULL(@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds,'') + CAST(@BuyOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(50))
				/**/
				END
				/*--------*/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
				SET [stsupIBuyOrderIds] = @stsupIBuyOrderIds
					,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] = @stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
				WHERE StockSupplyItemId = @StockSupplyItemId
				/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrStsupBordItem INTO	 @StockSupplyItemId,@stsupIPrdId,@stsupIQuantity
												,@stsupIDescription,@stsupIQuantityDesc,@stsupIPrdUnitId
												,@stsupIFCR,@stsupIFCRI,@stsupIWHId
												,@stsupITracing,@stsupIPrdUnitIdI,@stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@stsupIPrdColorId,@stsupIPrdNickname,@stsupILinkAccWHIdStr	
												,@stsupIBuyOrderIds,@stsupIBuyOrderItemIds
		END
		CLOSE CrsrStsupBordItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrStsupBordItem
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSBuyOrderItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts]
		@BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId,
		@Month1 = @Month1,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID			
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/		 
	/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END		
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup	
		SET @InsertedBuyOrderId = 0
		SET @InsertedBordNumber = 0	
		SET @FinalResult = 0	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup
		SET @FinalResult = 1	
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueBordFromStsup	
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @FinalResult AS 'Result'
/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END












GO

FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

BEGIN

	SET @CreateErrorMsg = ''
	SET @ErrorJson = ISNULL(@ErrorJson + ',','') + REPLACE((REPLACE(@SampleJson,'{0}',@ErrorId)),'{1}',@CreateErrorMsg)			
/**/
END
ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CompanyQuickRegisterDefSettingId,0) <> 0))*/
BEGIN
/**/
	/*----------------------------------------*/
	SELECT TOP (1) 
		 @CmpTitleId = ISNULL(@CmpTitleId,[cmpTitleId])
		,@cmpActivityType = [cmpActivityType]
		,@cmpGroupId = [cmpGroupId]

	IF(ISNULL(@cmpName,'') <> @LblPerson)
	BEGIN
	/**/		
		SET @DupCmpId = NULL
		SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
		SET @DupCmpName = NULL
		SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL
		SEt @DupCmpCity = NULL		

		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
		/**/	
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpTitleId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/	
				SELECT TOP(1) 
					 @DupCmpId = [CompanyID] 
					,@DupCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50))
					,@DupCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										 THEN (
												(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
												 ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''))
											  )
										 ELSE (
												SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
													   ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]	
											  )
									END)					
					,@DupCmpTitle = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										THEN (
												(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),''))
											 )
										ELSE (
												SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
											)				
									END)
					,@DupCmpCity = (SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = cmpCityId)
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
				WHERE ( 					   
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([cmpName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						=
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						)

				SELECT TOP(1) 
					 @DupCmpId = [CompanyID] 
					,@DupCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50))
					,@DupCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										 THEN (
												(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
												 ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''))
											  )
										 ELSE (
												SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
													   ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]	
											  )

					,@DupCmpTitle = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0

											 )

						=

												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP

						=
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						)

			/**/

					,@DupCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										 THEN (
												(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
												 ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))
											  )
										 ELSE (
												SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
													   ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'') FROm [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]	
											  )
									END)					
					,@DupCmpTitle = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										THEN (
												(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))
											 )
										ELSE (
												SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
											)				
									END)
					,@DupCmpCity = (SELECT City FROM [dbo].[City_Lang] CT WHERE CityId = cmpCityId  AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
				WHERE ( 					   
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),C.[cmpName])) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						=
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						)
				AND cmpCityId = @cmpCityId
				AND cmpTitleId = @CmpTitleId
			/**/
			END
			/*------------------------------*/			
		/**/
		END		
		/*-----------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@DupCmpId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ErrorId =  N'-10'/*dunlicate cmpName*/
			SET @ErrorIds = ISNULL(@ErrorIds + ',','') + @ErrorId 				
			SET @CreateErrorMsg = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@MsgErrorCheckcmpNameDuplicate,'{0}',ISNULL(@DupCmpName,''))),'{1}',ISNULL(@DupCmpTitle,'')))
										  ,'{2}',ISNULL(@DupCmpCity,''))),'{3}',ISNULL(@DupCmpNumber,''))
			SET @ErrorJson = ISNULL(@ErrorJson + ',','') + REPLACE((REPLACE(@SampleJson,'{0}',@ErrorId)),'{1}',@CreateErrorMsg)			
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*---- Person ----*/

		SET @prsLName_Lang = N'[{"LanguageId":1033,"Value":"Personel"},{"LanguageId":1025,"Value":"����"}]'

		SET @prsLName_Lang = @CompanyName_Lang
	/**/
	END

	IF(ISNULL(@PrsMobile,'') <> '')

			     @DupCmpId = [prjCompanyId]			  
				,@DupCmpName = (SELECT 
									(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
												  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
										   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
														ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
												)				
									 END)CompanyName				
								FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
								WHERE C.CompanyID = P.[prjCompanyId]) 				
			  ,@DupCmpNumber = CAST((SELECT [cmpNumber] FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE C.[CompanyId] = P.[prjCompanyId])  AS NVARCHAR(50))			                
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
		  WHERE P.[ProjectID] <> 0
		  AND REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([prjPostFix])),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
			  =

									(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
											THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
													ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
											ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
														ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
												)				
										END)CompanyName				
								FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
								WHERE C.CompanyID = P.[prjCompanyId]) 
			   ,@DupCmpNumber = CAST((SELECT [cmpNumber] FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE C.[CompanyId] = P.[prjCompanyId])  AS NVARCHAR(50))				         
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
		  WHERE P.[ProjectID] <> 0
		  AND (
			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM((SELECT [prjPostFix] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.[ProjectID] = P.[ProjectID] AND PLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID))),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), 
																NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
			  = 
			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM(@PrjPostFix)),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
																NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
			 OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([prjPostFix])),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), 
																NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
			  = 
			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM(@PrjPostFix)),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
																NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
			 )
		/**/
		END
		/*-----------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@DupCmpId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ErrorId =  N'-12'/*dunlicate prjPostFix*/

			SET @ErrorJson = ISNULL(@ErrorJson + ',','') + REPLACE((REPLACE(@SampleJson,'{0}',@ErrorId)),'{1}',@CreateErrorMsg)			
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@prjDesc,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@actNotCanSavePrjDescDup,0) = 1)

									(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
										   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
												  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
										   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
														ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
												)				
									 END)CompanyName				
								FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
								WHERE C.CompanyID = P.[prjCompanyId]) 				
			  ,@DupCmpNumber = CAST((SELECT [cmpNumber] FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE C.[CompanyId] = P.[prjCompanyId])  AS NVARCHAR(50))			                
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
		  WHERE P.[ProjectID] <> 0
		  AND REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([prjDesc])),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
			  = 
			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM(@prjDesc)),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)

		/**/
			SELECT TOP 1
			   @DupCmpId = [prjCompanyId]
			  ,@DupCmpName = (SELECT 
									(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
											THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
													ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
											ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
														ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
												WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
												)				
										END)CompanyName				
								FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
								WHERE C.CompanyID = P.[prjCompanyId]) 
			   ,@DupCmpNumber = CAST((SELECT [cmpNumber] FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE C.[CompanyId] = P.[prjCompanyId])  AS NVARCHAR(50))				         
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P

			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM((SELECT [prjDesc] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.[ProjectID] = P.[ProjectID] AND PLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID))),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), 
																NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
			  = 
			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM(@prjDesc)),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
																NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
			 OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([prjDesc])),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), 
																NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
			  = 
			  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM(@prjDesc)),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
																NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
			 )

		END

	END

BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
IF(ISNULL(@ErrorIds,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------- Company ------------------------*/
	DECLARE @InsertedCompany BIT = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*---------------------------------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
		SET @cmpNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[CRMfnReturnCompanyNumber]())

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany]
			([ImportDate]
			,[ImportDateTime]
			,[cmpNumber]
			,[cmpName]
			,[cmpTitleId]
			,[cmpActivityType]
			,[cmpGroupId]
			,[cmpSubGroupId]
			,[cmpGroupIdII]
			,[cmpGroupIdIII]
			,[cmpGroupIdIV]
			,[cmpTypeId]
			,[cmpPresentationId]			
			,[cmpMobile]
			,[cmpCountryId]
			,[cmpProvinceId]
			,[cmpCityId]
			,[cmpSpecialPoint]
			,[cmpDescription]
			,[cmpUserName]
			,[cmpPassword]
			,[cmpFirstRegisterUserId]
			,[UserId]
			,[Year1]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[cmpReferUserId]
			,[AppType]
			,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian]
			,[cmpEffectiveDateTime]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[cmpKind]
			,[cmpNameOfficial]
			/*RZ14020301- Delete cmpOfficialKind*/
			/*,[cmpOfficialKind]*/
			,[TspCmpTob]
			/*RZ14020301- End*/
			,[cmpOfficialCityId]
			,[cmpOfficialDesc])
		VALUES
			(@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@cmpNumber
			,@cmpName
			,@CmpTitleId
			,@cmpActivityType
			,@cmpGroupId
			,@cmpSubGroupId
			,@cmpGroupIdII
			,@cmpGroupIdIII
			,@cmpGroupIdIV
			,@cmpTypeId
			,@cmpPresentationId			
			,@cmpMobile
			,@cmpCountryId
			,@cmpProvinceId
			,@cmpCityId
			,@cmpSpecialPoint
			,@cmpDescription
			,@cmpUserName
			,@cmpPassword
			,@UserId
			,@UserId
			,@Year1
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian			
			,@UserId
			,@AppType
			,@cmpEffectiveDatePersian
			,@cmpEffectiveDateTime
			,@DefaultLanguageId
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate			
			,@cmpKind
			,@cmpName
			/*RZ14020301- Delete cmpOfficialKind*/
			/*,@cmpKind*/
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(@cmpKind,0) = 0 THEN 2 ELSE 1 END)
			/*RZ14020301- End*/
			,@cmpCityId
			,@cmpDescription)

		SET @CompanyId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		
		IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*---------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @InsertedCompany = 1
			/*MultiLanguage*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
				([CompanyID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[cmpName]
				,[cmpActivityType]
				,[cmpSpecialPoint]
				,[cmpDescription]
				,[cmpNameOfficial]
				,[cmpOfficialDesc])
			SELECT 
				 @CompanyId
				,[LanguageID]
				,ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Value] 
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@cmpName_Lang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[Value] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Value',
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId'
						) JS
						WHERE JS.LanguageId = CRD.LanguageID						
						),@cmpName)
				,[cmpActivityType]
				,[cmpSpecialPoint]
				,[cmpDescription]
				,ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Value] 
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@cmpName_Lang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[Value] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Value',
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId'
						) JS
						WHERE JS.LanguageId = CRD.LanguageID						
						),@cmpName)
				,[cmpDescription]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyQuickRegisterDefSetting_Lang] CRD
			WHERE [CompanyQuickRegisterDefSettingId] = @CompanyQuickRegisterDefSettingId
			AND CRD.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID				
			/*---------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END			

		/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into CrmCompany' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 		
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			
			SET @ErrorId =  N'-14' /*not insert company*/
			SET @ErrorIds = ISNULL(@ErrorIds + ',','') + @ErrorId 				
			SET @CreateErrorMsg = ''
			SET @ErrorJson = ISNULL(@ErrorJson + ',','') + REPLACE((REPLACE(@SampleJson,'{0}',@ErrorId)),'{1}',@CreateErrorMsg)
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/

		/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/

	/*------------------- Person ------------------------*/
	/*Find person*/
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0 )
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @CompanyPersonId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.CompanyPersonID 
						 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] P 
						 WHERE P.prsCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						 AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL(prsFName,'') + ISNULL(prsLName,'')) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))
						      = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(@PrsFName,'') + @prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))))
	/**/
	END
	/*----------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0 )
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*---------------------------------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/				

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
			([prsCompanyId]
			,[prsLName]
			,[prsTitleId]
			,[prsJobId]
			,[prsTypeId]
			,[prsGroupId]
			,[prsSexId]
			,[prsEducationId]			
			,[prsMobile]
			,[prsCountryId]
			,[prsProvinceId]
			,[prsCityId]
			,[prsSpecialPoint]
			,[prsDescription]
			,[UserId]
			,[Year1]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[ImportDate]
			,[ImportDateTime]
			,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]
			,[prsDepartmentId]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[prsFName]/*RZ14020128- Add prsFname and add conversation*/)
		VALUES
			(@CompanyId
			,@prsLName
			,@PrsTitleId
			,@prsJobId
			,@prsTypeId
			,@prsGroupId
			,@prsSexId
			,@prsEducationId			
			,@PrsMobile
			,@prsCountryId
			,@prsProvinceId
			,@prsCityId
			,@prsSpecialPoint
			,@prsDescription
			,@UserId
			,@Year1
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate

						WITH(
							[Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value',

						) JS
						WHERE JS.LanguageId = CRD.LanguageID						
						),@prsLName)				   
				   ,[prsSpecialPoint]
				   ,[prsDescription]

				   ,ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Value]

		/*------------------- tran5 --------------------*/
		/*---------------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------- OrderItems ------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@OrderId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorIds,'') = '')

			/*---------------------------------------------------*/

			/*------------------- tran6 --------------------*/				   		 		
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/						   			 		  

			DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL,@ordIPrdId INT = NULL,
			@ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,@ordICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
			@ordIOrderView INT = NULL,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
			@ordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@ordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
			@ordIWHId INT = NULL,@ordIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@ordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @ordIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL,
			@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@ordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,
			@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
			@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
			@ordIPrdColorId INT = NULL,@ordIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,
			@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL,@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
			@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
			/**/
			@CrsrOrdIPrdId INT = NULL,@CrsrOrdIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
			@CrsrOrdIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@CrsrOrdIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
			@CrsrOrdIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@CrsrOrdIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL,
			@CrsrOrdIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@CrsrOrdIFCRI FLOAT = NULL
			
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/	
			DECLARE CrsrissueQuickOrdItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
			/**/				
				SELECT 
					 [ordIPrdId]
					,[ordIQuantity]
					,[ordIPrice]
					,[ordIDiscount]
					,[ordIDiscountPercent]
					,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
					,[ordIFCR]
					,[ordIFCRI]
				FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@OrderItemJson,'[]'))
				WITH(
					ordIPrdId INT '$.ordIPrdId',
					ordIQuantity FLOAT '$.ordIQuantity',
					ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) '$.ordIPrice',
					ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) '$.ordIDiscount',
					ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) '$.ordIDiscountPercent',
					ordIPrdPriceGroupID INT '$.ordIPrdPriceGroupID',
					ordIFCR FLOAT '$.ordIFCR',
					ordIFCRI FLOAT '$.ordIFCRI'
				)
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrissueQuickOrdItem
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrissueQuickOrdItem INTO @CrsrOrdIPrdId,@CrsrOrdIQuantity,
													   @CrsrOrdIPrice,@CrsrOrdIDiscount,
													   @CrsrOrdIDiscountPercent,@CrsrOrdIPrdPriceGroupID,
													   @CrsrOrdIFCR,@CrsrOrdIFCRI
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------ set fields ---------------------*/
				SET @OrderItemID = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdId = @CrsrOrdIPrdId
				SET @ordIQuantity = @CrsrOrdIQuantity
				SET @ordIPrice = @CrsrOrdIPrice
				SET @ordITotalPrice = NULL
				SET @ordIDiscount = @CrsrOrdIDiscount
				SET @ordIDiscountPercent = @CrsrOrdIDiscountPercent
				SET @ordIPrdUnitId = NULL
				SET @ordICurrencyUnitId = @ordCurrencyUnitId
				SET @ordIOrderView = NULL
				SET @ordIFCR = @CrsrOrdIFCR
				SET @ordITax = NULL
				SET @ordITaxIndex  = NULL
				SET @ordICalculateTax = NULL
				SET @ordIFCRI = @CrsrOrdIFCRI
				SET @ordIWHId = @ordDefWHId
				SET @ordIRound = NULL
				SET @ordIRoundPlus = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdPriceGroupID = ISNULL(@CrsrOrdIPrdPriceGroupID,@ordDefPrdPriceGroupId)
				SET @ordIPriceSum = NULL
				SET @ordIDiscountSum = NULL
				SET @ordITaxSum = NULL
				SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = NULL
				SET @ordIValidityExpireDatePersian = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdUnitIdI = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdColorId = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdValidityCountingNum = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdValidityCountingMin = NULL
				SET @ordIPrdNickname = NULL
				SET @ordIDescription = NULL

				
				/*----------------------*/
				DECLARE @PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,@IsCalcValidityExpireDate BIT = NULL
				,@PrdValidityTimeHour INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTimeMinute INT = NULL,@PrdValidity BIT = NULL
				,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT = NULL
				,@PrdNoDiscount BIT = NULL, @PrdDiscountMax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@PrdDiscountMaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
				,@DiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
				@ordIDiscountSumExtra NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ItemCount INT = NULL
				SELECT TOP (1) 					
					 @ordIPrdColorId = [PrdColorId]
					/*,@ordIPrdUnitId = [PrdUnitId]*/
					,@ordIDescription = [PrdDescription]					
					,@PrdValidity = [PrdValidity]
					,@PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
					,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]
					/*,@ordIPrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI]*/
					,@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate]
					,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto]
					,@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]					
					,@ordIPrdNickname = [PrdNickname]
					,@PrdNoDiscount = [PrdNoDiscount]
					,@PrdDiscountMax = [PrdDiscountMax]	
					,@ordIPrdValidityCountingNum = [PrdValidityCountingNum]
					,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin = [PrdValidityCountingMin]
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE [ProductID] = @ordIPrdId
				/*---------*/
				/*IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitId,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/-**-/
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIFCR,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/-**-/
						SELECT TOP (1)
							 @ordIFCR = [FCR]							
							,@ordIFCRI = [FCRI]						
						FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits]
						WHERE [ProductUnitID] = @ordIPrdUnitId
					/-**-/
					END
				/-**-/
				END*/
				/*---------*/				
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdPriceGroupID,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice])					
				/**/
				END
				/*-------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
				/*------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
					SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100					
				/**/
				END				
				ELSE
				BEGIN
				/**/	
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) * 100) / (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
						SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*------------------------*/
				/*--- Check Discount max ---*/				
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdDiscountMax,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @PrdDiscountMaxSum = ISNULL(@PrdDiscountMax,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
					SET @ordIDiscountSumExtra = @ordIDiscountSum
					IF(ISNULL(@PrdDiscountMaxSum,0) < ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0))
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@PrdDiscountMaxSum,0)
						IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) * 100) / (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
							SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
						/**/
						END
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END		
				/*------------------------*/				
				/*OrderView*/
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
				SET @ordIOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 4 ) - 1
				/*---------------- Calculate item ----------------*/
				SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					/*RZ14021003- Change Round to use function and add parameters*/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))					
					/*RZ14021003- End*/
				END	
				
				SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0)

				SET @ordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
				SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)

				SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = @PrdUnitIRateResult
				/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					/*RZ14021003- Change Round to use function and add parameters*/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))
					SET @ordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscount))
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
					SET @ordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITotalPrice))
					/*RZ14021003- End*/
				END				
				/*------------------------*/
				
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					([OrderId]
					,[ordIPrdId]
					,[ordIQuantity]
					,[ordIPrice]
					,[ordITotalPrice]
					,[ordIDescription]
					,[UserId]
					,[FPId]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[ordIDiscount]
					,[ordIDiscountPercent]
					,[ordIPrdUnitId]
					,[ordICurrencyUnitId]

			@FPId = @FPId,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@AppType = @AppType, 
			@LanguageID = @DefLanguageID,
			@InsertedId = @DiscountCodeHistoryInsertedId OUTPUT
		/**/
		
		DECLARE @ScoresInsertedId INT = 0,
				@Score FLOAT = 0,
				@ScoreCmpId INT = 0
		SET @Year1 = CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
		SET @Score = (SELECT TOP (1) [ScoreRate] FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode] WHERE [DiscountCodeId] = @DiscountCodeId)
		SET @ScoreCmpId = (SELECT TOP (1) [ordCompanyId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId)
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@Score,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Score = ISNULL(@Score,0) * -1
			EXEC ScorespInsertScores 
			@ScoreDate = @RegisterDate,
			@ScoreDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@Score = @Score,
			@ScoreRuleId = NULL,
			@ScoreOrderId = NULL,
			@ScoreCmpId = @ScoreCmpId,
			@OrderView = NULL,
			@ScoreSysPartId = 80,/*DisCode*/
			@ScoreObjectId = @DiscountCodeId,
			@ScoreOprType = 2,/*Spend*/
			@ScoreExpireDate = NULL,
			@ScoreExpireDatePersian = NULL,
			@ScoreIsExpired  = NULL,
			/**/
			@UserId = @UserId,
			@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@Year1 = @Year1,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@AppType = @AppType, 
			@LanguageID = @DefLanguageID,
			@InsertedId = @ScoresInsertedId OUTPUT
		/**/
		END		
	/**/
	END
	/*!NKR14021004*/

	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/

IF(ISNULL(@OrderId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorNumber,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorIds,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
/**/
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	/*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	SET @OrderId = NULL
	SET @ordNumber = NULL	
/**/
END

SELECT @CompanyId [CompanyId],
	   @CompanyPersonId [CompanyPersonId], 
	   @ProjectId [ProjectId],
	   @OrderId [OrderId],
	   @ordNumber [OrderNumber],
	   @ErrorIds [ErrorIds],
	   ISNULL('[' + @ErrorJson + ']','') [ErrorJson]
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspIssueDocFromBuyOrder] 
(		
/*
	exec WSspIssueDocFromBuyOrder  18, '13890714', 3, 7, 1, '13890714'
*/
@BuyOrderID INT,
@DocDate VARCHAR(8),
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@WHId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50), 
@InsertedDocID INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedDocNumber INT OUTPUT,
/*ADBF951130*/
@LanguageID INT ,
@BordItemList NVARCHAR(MAX)/* = NULL*//*RZ14000631- manual quantity for every product in multi doc*/ /*RZ14000908- Remove null in parameters*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------- Set TmpTabel -------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14000631- Add manual quantity for every product in multi doc*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
	[BuyOrderItemId] INT,
	[Quantity] FLOAT
)
IF(ISNULL(@BordItemList,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CrsrBuyOrderItemId INT,@CrsrQuantity FLOAT,@Text NVARCHAR(MAX)
	/**/
	DECLARE CrsrI CURSOR LOCAL FOR
	/**/	
		SELECT SplitData  
		FROM fnSplitString(@BordItemList, '|')
	/**/
	OPEN CrsrI
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*================================================================================*/
		DECLARE @Field NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		SET @CrsrBuyOrderItemId = NULL
		SET @CrsrQuantity = NULL			
		/**/
		/*****************************************************************/
		IF(ISNULL(@Text,'') <> '') 
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*===========================================================*/	
			SET @CrsrBuyOrderItemId = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, 1, CHARINDEX(',',@Text)-1),'0') AS INT)                                    
			SET @CrsrQuantity = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, CHARINDEX(',', @Text)+1, Len(@Text)),'0') AS FLOAT)

			INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([BuyOrderItemId],[Quantity])
			VALUES(@CrsrBuyOrderItemId,@CrsrQuantity)
								
			/*===========================================================*/				
		/**/
		END
		/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
	/**/	
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	END
	CLOSE CrsrI
	DEALLOCATE CrsrI
/**/
END
/*RZ14000631- End*/

/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldDocID INT
/*SELECT @InsertedOldDocID = DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc Doc WHERE Doc.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId 
IF @InsertedOldDocID > 0 RETURN -1*/

/*RZ14000631- Add manual quantity for every product in multi doc*/
/*���� ���� ���� � �ǘ��� ����� �� ����� ���� �ѐ� ����� ���� ����  �� ������ ������ ����� �����  ����� �� ���.
 ���� �� ���� �ѐ� ����� �� ����� ������ ���� �� �ǘ��� ���� ���.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(bordIQuantity,0)) 
			FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItems 
			WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID
			/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
			AND bordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
			/*RZ14020606- End*/
			),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(Quantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID)),0)
	),0) = 0
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END
/*RZ14000631*/

*/
