/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
DECLARE @DocDateTime DATETIME, @TimeNow TIME
SET @DocDateTime = (SELECT dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DocDate, null))
SET @TimeNow = CONVERT(TIME, GETDATE())
SET @DocDateTime = @DocDateTime + CAST(@TimeNow AS DATETIME)
/*RZ980703- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @DocNumber INT
/*Select @DocNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderOpr op WHERE op.BuyOrderOprID = 
(SELECT BuyOrderOprId FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId) ) )
SET @InsertedDocNumber = @DocNumber*/ /*RZ14000631*/
/*
---------------------------------------------------
--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
/*RZ960225 dalile jabejaie declare @DocId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be docid jadid niaz darim*/
Declare @DocId INT  

BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14000631- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @WareHouseId INT
	DECLARE Crsr_Wrhs CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			bordiWHId
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
		WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID 
		AND BuyOrderItemId IN(SELECT TT.BuyOrderItemId FROM @TmpTable TT WHERE ISNULL(TT.Quantity,0) > 0)	
		/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
		AND bordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
							  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
							  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
		/*RZ14020606- End*/
	OPEN Crsr_Wrhs
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN		
		SET @DocId = NULL
		SET @InsertedDocID = NULL
		SET @DocNumber = NULL
		SET @InsertedDocNumber = NULL
		/*---------------- Doc Number ----------------*/				
		Select @DocNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
		(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderOpr op WHERE op.BuyOrderOprID = 
		(SELECT BuyOrderOprId FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId) ) )
		SET @InsertedDocNumber = @DocNumber
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------*/
		INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
			OprId,
			DocNumber,
			DocDate,
			DocDateTime,
			BuyOrderId,
			SendAddress, 
			IsFix,	
			WHId,
			UserId,	
			Month1,	
			FPId, 
			RegisterDate, 
			RegisterDatePersian, 
			FirstRegisterUserId, 
			FirstRegisterDate, 
			CompanyId,
			CompanyPersonId,
			ProjectId,
			AppType,
			/*ADBF951130*/
			LanguageID,
			FirstRegisterDatePersian 
		) 	
		SELECT  
			(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderOpr op WHERE op.BuyOrderOprID = BO.BuyOrderOprId), 
			@DocNumber,
			@DocDate, 
			/*GETDATE(),*/
			@DocDateTime,/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
			BuyOrderId,
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
				(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = bordCompanyId)) 
			+ ' '+
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
				(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = bordCompanyId)) 
			+ ' '+
			(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = bordCompanyId), 
			1,
			/*@WhID,*/
			ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhId),/*RZ14000631- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
			@UserId, 
			@Month1, 
			@FPId, 
			GETDATE(), 
			@RegisterDatePersian, 
			@UserId,
			GETDATE(), 
			bordCompanyId,
			bordCompanyPersonId,
			bordProjectId,
			@AppType,
			/*ADBF951130*/
			@LanguageID, 
			@RegisterDatePersian
		FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder BO 
		WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId

		/*RZ960225 for multi language And add length variable*/
		/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
		/*RZ960225 dar inja nesbat dehi be @DocId anjam mishavad*/
		Select @DocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
			(
			   [DocID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[SendAddress]
			 )
		SELECT 
			@DocId,
			BOL.LanguageID,
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
				(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = BO.bordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = BOL.LanguageID) 
			+ ' '+
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN  WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
				(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = BO.bordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = BOL.LanguageID) 
			+ ' '+
			(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = BO.bordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = BOL.LanguageID)
		FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder_Lang BOL 
		INNER JOIN dbo.WSBuyOrder BO ON BOL.BuyOrderId = BO.BuyOrderId
		WHERE BO.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
		/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/

		/*------------------- tran1---------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSDoc' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*------------------- tran1-------------------------*/
		/*===================================================================
		  ===================================================================
		  ===================================================================*/
		/*Declare @DocId INT 
		Select @DocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()*/
		/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
		/*RZ960225 dalile jabejaie declare @DocId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be DocId jadid niaz darim*/
		/*chon dar zaman darj doc be @DocId id jadid nesbat dade shode inja be 2 dastor bala niazi nist*/
		SET @InsertedDocID = @DocId
		/*-----------------------------------------------------
		------------------- tran2*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran2*/
		DECLARE @BuyOrderItemID INT = NULL, @bordIPrdId INT = NULL, @bordIQuantity float = NULL , 
		/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/@bordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @bordIWHId INT = NULL
		,@bordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@bordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
		,@bordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
		,@bordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
		 ,@bordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL/*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
		 /*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
		,@bordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL
		,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT  = NULL
		,@TmpI FLOAT = NULL
		/*RZ14010621- End*/
		,@bordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
		/*--*/
		DECLARE crsr CURSOR FOR /*For OrderItems*/
		SELECT 	BuyOrderItemID,
			bordIPrdId,
			bordIQuantity,
			bordIDescription
			,bordIFCR,bordIFCRI/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
			,bordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			,bordIPrdUnitId/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
			,bordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,bordIPrdUnitIdI
			,bordIPrdUnitIRateResult
			/*RZ14010621- End*/
			,bordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
		FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItems
		WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
		AND bordIWHId = @WareHouseId /*RZ14000631- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
		/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
		AND bordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
		/*RZ14020606- End*/
		/*--*/
		OPEN crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @BuyOrderItemID, @bordIPrdId, @bordIQuantity, 
									@bordIDescription,@bordIFCR,@bordIFCRI/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
									,@bordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
									,@bordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
									,@bordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
									,@bordIPrdUnitIdI,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
									,@bordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
		/*--*/
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 
		BEGIN
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------------- WSDocItems --------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14000631- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
			IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM @TmpTable))
			BEGIN
			/**/
				DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
				SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP 1 [Quantity] FROM @TmpTable WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemID)
				IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @BuyOrderItemID, @bordIPrdId, @bordIQuantity, 
									@bordIDescription,@bordIFCR,@bordIFCRI,@bordITracing,@bordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
									,@bordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
									,@bordIPrdUnitIdI,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
									,@bordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					CONTINUE
				/**/
				END
				ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*RZ14011005- Calculate QuantityTotal*/
					IF(ISNULL(@bordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@bordIQuantity,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrQty,0))
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @bordIQuantityTotal = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) * ISNULL(@bordIFCR,1)
					/**/
					END
					/*RZ14011005- End*/

					SET @bordIQuantity = @CurrQty
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14000631- End*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/			
			/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
			SET @TmpI = NULL
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @bordIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@bordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@bordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@bordIFCRI,1)
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@bordIQuantity,1) 					
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*SET @bordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
				SET @bordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@bordIQuantity,1)/*RZ14010629- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

				IF(ISNULL(@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @bordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14010621- End*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems (
				DocId,
				PrdId,
				Quantity,
				[Description],
				/*IsFix,*/
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian,
				FirstRegisterUserId,
				FirstRegisterDate 
				,[FCR],[FCRI]/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
				,[Tracing]/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/ 
				,[PrdUnitId] /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
				,[QuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
				/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
				,[PrdUnitIdI]
				,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
				/*RZ14010621- End*/	
				,[PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				,[BuyOrderItemId] /*RZ14021202- Add BuyOrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
			) 
			VALUES(
				@DocId,
				@bordIPrdId,
				@bordIQuantity,
				@bordIDescription,
				/*@WHId,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WHId),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				@LanguageID,
				@RegisterDatePersian,
				@UserId,
				GETDATE() 
				,@bordIFCR,@bordIFCRI/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
				,@bordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
				,@bordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
				,@bordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
				/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
				,@bordIPrdUnitIdI
				,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult
				/*RZ14010621- End*/	
				,@bordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				,@BuyOrderItemID /*RZ14021202- Add BuyOrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
			)
			/*--*/
			Declare @DocItemId INT = NULL
			Select @DocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

			/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
			/*Begin insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems_Lang (
				[DocItemID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Description]	
				,[PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			) 
			SELECT
				@DocItemId,
				BOILN.LanguageID,
				BOILN.bordIDescription,
				BOILN.bordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItems_Lang BOILN 
			WHERE  BOILN.BuyOrderItemID = @BuyOrderItemID
			/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
			/*End insert records in lang table*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------------- WSDocItemSerials --------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
			
			BEGIN TRAN
			/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
			/*RZ960225 for multi language */
			/*----------------- Begin user cursor for insert serial in doc -------------------*/
			/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @BuyOrderItemSerialID INT = NULL
			DECLARE @Serial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
			DECLARE @StatusId INT = NULL
			/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
			DECLARE @bordItemSerialDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
			DECLARE @IsFix SMALLINT = NULL
			DECLARE @SerialId INT = NULL
			DECLARE @IsAutoBarcode BIT = NULL
			DECLARE @tblTmpBarcodeId INT = NULL
			DECLARE @ExpDate DATETIME = NULL
			DECLARE @ExpDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
			DECLARE @InfoI NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			DECLARE @InfoII NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			DECLARE @InfoIII NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			DECLARE @InfoIV NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			DECLARE @InfoV NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			DECLARE @InfoVI NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			DECLARE @docItemSerialId INT = NULL
			/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
			Declare Crsr_Add_Serial CURSOR LOCAL For
				/*RZ14000631- Insert docItemSerials to number  (@bordIQuantity)*/
				SELECT TOP (CAST(@bordIQuantity AS INT))/*RZ14000631- Add manual quantity for every product in multi doc*/
					BuyOrderItemSerialID,
					Serial,
					StatusId,
					[Description],
					IsFix,
					SerialId,
					IsAutoBarcode, 
					tblTmpBarcodeId,
					ExpDate, 
					ExpDatePersian,
					InfoI, 
					InfoII, 
					InfoIII, 
					InfoIV, 
					InfoV, 
					InfoVI	
				FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItemSerials OSI
				WHERE BuyOrderItemID = @BuyOrderItemID	
				/*RZ14000631- Add manual quantity for every product in multi doc*/
				
				AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((OSI.Serial),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
									NOT IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DIS.Serial AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
											WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																WHERE PrdId = @bordIPrdId
																AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																				WHERE D.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID
																				/*AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1)*/)																
																)																
						)
						
				/*RZ14000628- End*/
					
				OPEN Crsr_Add_Serial
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO @BuyOrderItemSerialID, @Serial, @StatusId, @bordItemSerialDescription,
														@IsFix, @SerialId, @IsAutoBarcode, @tblTmpBarcodeId, @ExpDate, @ExpDatePersian,
														@InfoI, @InfoII, @InfoIII, @InfoIV, @InfoV, @InfoVI
	
				WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
				BEGIN
				/*---------------------------------------*/
					/*RZ14000631- Add rootId and Serial*/
					DECLARE @FindSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@RootId INT = NULL,@OprType INT = NULL
					/*Find doc type via orderOpr*/
					SET @OprType = (SELECT TOP 1[OprType]
										FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr]  DO
										WHERE DO.[DocOprID]  = ((SELECT  
																	(SELECT TOP 1 Opr.DocOprId 
																		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderOpr] Opr WHERE Opr.BuyOrderOprID = O.BuyOrderOprId) 			 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] O 
																	WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderID))						
									)									
					/*check doc type*/
					IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 1)/*Doc*/
					BEGIN
					/**/						
						SET @RootId = 0
						SET @SerialId = NULL
					/**/
					END
					ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 2 OR  ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)/*Draft Or Movement*/
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*----------------*/
						SELECT TOP 1 
							 @SerialId = [DocItemSerialID]							
							,@RootId = [RootId]
						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
						WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemId] 
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
											WHERE DI.DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId
																FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D
																/*WHERE D.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID*/
																WHERE D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN(1,3))
																AND D.WHId = ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WHId))
											)
						AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([Serial] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) = 
							(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Serial AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))							
						SET @RootId = ISNULL(@RootId,0)
						IF(@RootId = 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @RootId = @SerialId
						END							
						/*----------------*/
					/**/
					END						 			
						
					/*RZ14000325- End*/

					INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItemSerials (
						ItemId,
						Serial,
						StatusId,
						[Description],
						IsFix, 
						WHId, 
						UserId, 
						Month1, 
						FPId, 
						RegisterDate, 
						RegisterDatePersian,
						SerialId, 
						IsAutoBarcode, 
						tblTmpBarcodeId,
						ExpDate, 
						ExpDatePersian, 
						AppType, 
						InfoI, 
						InfoII, 
						InfoIII, 
						InfoIV, 
						InfoV, 
						InfoVI,
						/*ADBF951130*/
						LanguageID,
						FirstRegisterDatePersian,
						FirstRegisterUserId,
						FirstRegisterDate ,
						[RootId]/*RZ14000325- Add rootId*/	
						
					)	
					VALUES( 
						@DocItemId,
						@Serial,
						@StatusId,
						@bordItemSerialDescription,
						@IsFix,
						/*@WHId,*/
						ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WHId),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
						@UserId, 
						@Month1, 
						@FPId, 
						GETDATE(), 
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@SerialId,
						@IsAutoBarcode, 
						@tblTmpBarcodeId,
						@ExpDate, 
						@ExpDatePersian, 
						@AppType, 
						@InfoI, 
						@InfoII, 
						@InfoIII, 
						@InfoIV, 
						@InfoV, 
						@InfoVI,
						/*ADBF951130*/
						@LanguageID,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@UserId,
						GETDATE() ,
						/*0*//*RZ14000325- Add rootId*/									
						@RootId/*RZ14000631- Add multi doc*/						
					)
					SET @docItemSerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
					/*Insert records in lang table*/			
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
					(
						[DocItemSerialID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[Description]
						,InfoI 
						,InfoII
						,InfoIII 
						,InfoIV
						,InfoV 
						,InfoVI
					)
					SELECT
						@docItemSerialId,
						BOISLN.LanguageID,
						BOISLN.[Description],
						BOISLN.InfoI, 
						BOISLN.InfoII, 
						BOISLN.InfoIII, 
						BOISLN.InfoIV, 
						BOISLN.InfoV, 
						BOISLN.InfoVI			
					FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItemSerials_Lang BOISLN 
					WHERE  BOISLN.BuyOrderItemSerialID = @BuyOrderItemSerialID
		
					FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO  @BuyOrderItemSerialID, @Serial, @StatusId, @bordItemSerialDescription,
															@IsFix, @SerialId, @IsAutoBarcode, @tblTmpBarcodeId, @ExpDate, @ExpDatePersian,
															@InfoI, @InfoII, @InfoIII, @InfoIV, @InfoV, @InfoVI
				/*---------------------------------------*/
				END
				CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
				DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
				/*----------------- End user cursor for insert serial in doc --------------------------------*/
				/*-------------------------------------- Old version ----------------------------------------*/
				/*
	
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItemSerials (
					ItemId,
					Serial,
					StatusId,
					Description,
					IsFix, 
					WHId, 
					UserId, Month1, FPId, RegisterDate, RegisterDatePersian,
					SerialId, 
					IsAutoBarcode, tblTmpBarcodeId,
					ExpDate, ExpDatePersian, AppType, 
					InfoI, InfoII, InfoIII, InfoIV, InfoV, InfoVI,
					/*ADBF951130*/
					LanguageID,
					FirstRegisterDatePersian,
					FirstRegisterUserId,
					FirstRegisterDate 	
				)	
				SELECT 
					@DocItemId,
					Serial,
					StatusId,
					Description,
					IsFix,
					@WHId,
					@UserId, @Month1, @FPId, GETDATE(), @RegisterDatePersian,
					SerialId,
					IsAutoBarcode, tblTmpBarcodeId,
					ExpDate, ExpDatePersian, @AppType, 
					InfoI, InfoII, InfoIII, InfoIV, InfoV, InfoVI,
					/*ADBF951130*/
					@LanguageID,
					@RegisterDatePersian,
					@UserId,
					GETDATE() 		
				FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItemSerials 
				WHERE BuyOrderItemID = @BuyOrderItemID	*/
				/*------------------- tran3*/
				IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN 
					SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItemSerial' 
					SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
					/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
					CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
					DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
				ROLLBACK TRAN 
				END		
				ELSE 
				COMMIT TRAN 
				/*------------------- tran3 ----------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @BuyOrderItemID, @bordIPrdId, @bordIQuantity, @bordIDescription,@bordIFCR,@bordIFCRI/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */ 
										,@bordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
										,@bordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
										,@bordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
										,@bordIPrdUnitIdI,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
										,@bordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			END
			/*--*/
			CLOSE crsr
			DEALLOCATE crsr
		/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Wrhs
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Wrhs
		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14000631- End*/

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	Set @DocId = 0
	Return @DocId
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	Return @DocId
END	
/*--***/
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[WSspIssueDraftFromOrder]






/*--891115921115930994119502*/
/*RZ960225 For multi language*/
/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
/*RZ980710- Move OrdDescII to Doc*/
/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
/*RZ14000908- Remove null in parameters*/
/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
/*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
/*RZ14010629- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/
/*RZ14011005- Calculate QuantityTotal*/
/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
/*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspIssueDraftFromOrder]	
(		
/*
	declare @InsertedDraftID INT = 0, @InsertedDraftNumber INT = 0
	exec WSspIssueDraftFromOrder  
	@OrderID = 17613,
	@DraftDate = '14000226',
	@UserId  = 3,
	@Month1 = 2,
	@FPId = 30,
	@WHId = 1,
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14000226',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@InsertedDraftID = @InsertedDraftID OUTPUT ,
	@InsertedDraftNumber = @InsertedDraftNumber OUTPUT,	
	@LanguageID = 1065,
	@OrdItemList = N'3113,5|3114,3|3115,2|3116,0|3117,4|3118,4|3120,0'
	select @InsertedDraftID,@InsertedDraftNumber
	 
*/
@OrderID INT,
@DraftDate VARCHAR(8),
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@WHId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@InsertedDraftID INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedDraftNumber INT OUTPUT,
/*ADBF951130*/
@LanguageID INT ,
@OrdItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) /*= NULL*//*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/ /*RZ14000908- Remove null in parameters*/
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------- Set TmpTabel -------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
	[OrderItemId] INT,
	[Quantity] FLOAT
)
IF(ISNULL(@OrdItemList,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CrsrOrderItemId INT,@CrsrQuantity FLOAT,@Text NVARCHAR(MAX)
	/**/
	DECLARE CrsrI CURSOR LOCAL FOR
	/**/	
		SELECT SplitData  
		FROM fnSplitString(@OrdItemList, '|')
	/**/
	OPEN CrsrI
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*================================================================================*/
		DECLARE @Field NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		SET @CrsrOrderItemId = NULL
		SET @CrsrQuantity = NULL			
		/**/
		/*****************************************************************/
		IF(ISNULL(@Text,'') <> '') 
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*===========================================================*/	
			SET @CrsrOrderItemId = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, 1, CHARINDEX(',',@Text)-1),'0') AS INT)                                    
			SET @CrsrQuantity = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, CHARINDEX(',', @Text)+1, Len(@Text)),'0') AS FLOAT)

			INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([OrderItemId],[Quantity])
			VALUES(@CrsrOrderItemId,@CrsrQuantity)
								
			/*===========================================================*/				
		/**/
		END
		/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
	/**/	
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	END
	CLOSE CrsrI
	DEALLOCATE CrsrI
/**/
END
/*RZ14000628- End*/

/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldDraftID INT
/*SELECT @InsertedOldDraftID = DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc Doc WHERE Doc.OrderId = @OrderID
IF @InsertedOldDraftID > 0 RETURN -1*/ 
/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
/*���� ���� ���� � �ǘ��� ����� �� ����� ���� �ѐ� ����� ���� ����  �� ������ ������ ����� �����  ����� �� ���.
 ���� �� ���� �ѐ� ����� �� ����� ������ ���� �� �ǘ��� ���� ���.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ordIQuantity,0)) 
			FROM dbo.WSOrderItems 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
			/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
			AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
			/*RZ14020606- End*/
			),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(Quantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderID)),0)
	),0) = 0
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END
/*RZ14000628*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
DECLARE @DocDateTime DATETIME, @TimeNow TIME
SET @DocDateTime = (SELECT dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DraftDate, null))
SET @TimeNow = CONVERT(TIME, GETDATE())
SET @DocDateTime = @DocDateTime + CAST(@TimeNow AS DATETIME)
/*RZ980703- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @DraftNumber INT
/*Select @DraftNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = 
(SELECT OrderOprId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId) ) )
SET @InsertedDraftNumber = @DraftNumber*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ980710- Move OrdDescII to Doc*/
DECLARE @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc INT
IF EXISTS (
SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromOrder' )
AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actMoveOrdDescriptionIIToDoc'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromOrder' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 1
ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 0
	
/*RZ980710- END*/

/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
/*RZ960225 dalile jabejaie declare @DraftId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be draftid jadid niaz darim*/
Declare @DraftId INT 
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @WareHouseId INT
	DECLARE Crsr_Wrhs CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			ordiWHId
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderID 
		/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
		AND OrderItemID IN(SELECT TT.OrderItemId FROM @TmpTable TT WHERE ISNULL(TT.Quantity,0) > 0)
		/*RZ14000628- End*/		
		/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
		AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
							WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
		/*RZ14020606- End*/
	OPEN Crsr_Wrhs
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN		
		SET @DraftId = NULL
		SET @InsertedDraftID = NULL
		SET @DraftNumber = NULL
		SET @InsertedDraftNumber = NULL
		/*---------------- Doc Number ----------------*/
		SELECT @DraftNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
								(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = 
								(SELECT OrderOprId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId) ) )
		SET @InsertedDraftNumber = @DraftNumber
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------- tran1 ----------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran1*/
		/*RZ980710- Move OrdDescII to Doc*/	
		IF @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 1
		BEGIN
		/*---------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
				OprId,
				DocNumber,
				DocDate,
				DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				/*--BuyOrderId,*/
				SendAddress, 
				IsFix,	
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				FirstRegisterUserId, 
				FirstRegisterDate, 
				CompanyId,
				CompanyPersonId,
				ProjectId, 
				AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian 
				,[Description]
			) 	
			SELECT  
				(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID), 
				@DraftNumber,
				@DraftDate,
				/*GETDATE(),*/
				@DocDateTime,/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
				OrderId,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId), 
				1,
				/*@WhID,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@UserId,
				GETDATE(), 
				ordCompanyId,
				ordCompanyPersonId,
				ordProjectId, 
				@AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				@LanguageID, 
				@RegisterDatePersian
				,[ordDescriptionII]		 
			FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
			/*RZ960225 for multi language And add length variable*/
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			/*RZ960225 dar inja nesbat dehi be @DraftId anjam mishavad*/
			SELECT @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
				(
				   [DocID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[SendAddress]
				   ,[Description]
				 )
			SELECT 
				@DraftId,
				OL.LanguageID,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID)
				,OL.[ordDescriptionII]
			FROM dbo.WSOrder_Lang OL 
			INNER JOIN dbo.WSOrder O ON OL.OrderId = O.OrderId
			WHERE O.OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/ 
		/*---------------------------------*/
		END/*RZ980710- END*/
		ELSE IF @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 0
		BEGIN
		/*---------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
				OprId,
				DocNumber,
				DocDate,
				DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				/*--BuyOrderId,*/
				SendAddress, 
				IsFix,	
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				FirstRegisterUserId, 
				FirstRegisterDate, 
				CompanyId,
				CompanyPersonId,
				ProjectId, 
				AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian 
			) 	
			SELECT  
				(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID), 
				@DraftNumber,
				@DraftDate,
				/*GETDATE(),*/
				@DocDateTime,/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
				OrderId,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId), 
				1,
				/*@WhID,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@UserId,
				GETDATE()
